### [67] [Probing the classical complexity of quantum dynamics experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31830)
*Thomas Schuster,Andreas Elben*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"反应性(reacitivity)"这一新的复杂度度量,用于刻画近期发现的经典可高效模拟量子电路/动力学的方法,并给出实验协议测量该量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期工作表明,许多纠缠和magic等传统复杂度度量很高的量子电路与动力学,仍可被追踪局部信息的经典算法高效模拟。传统复杂度度量(如纠缠、magic)无法捕捉这种新的经典攻击方式,因为它们刻画的是量子态或算符孤立的性质,而非量子实验整体。

Method: 引入反应性(reacitivity)作为量子实验整体的复杂度度量,而非单个量子态或算符的性质;并通过数值与严格证据分析低反应性实验的可模拟性、可学习性与可加速性。进一步设计"Pauli路径谱学(Pauli path spectroscopy)"实验协议,以高效测量任意量子实验的反应性。

Result: 低反应性的量子实验在多重意义上都是"简单"的:可被经典高效模拟、可学习、可快速前向计算(fast-forward)。Pauli路径谱学协议可高效测量任意量子实验的反应性,即使该实验本身已超出经典模拟能力范围。

Conclusion: 反应性提供了一个超越传统复杂度度量的新视角,刻画了量子实验整体的经典可处理性。寻找高反应性的量子实验可能规避这些简化特征,而Pauli路径谱学为实验上探测反应性提供了可行手段。

Abstract: A confluence of recent works has shown that many quantum circuits and dynamics are efficiently simulable by classical algorithms that track local information, even when conventional complexity measures such as the entanglement and magic are high. Here, we introduce a novel measure of complexity, the reactivity, to capture this new method of classical attack. Unlike conventional complexity measures, the reactivity does not capture a property of a quantum state or operator in isolation, but rather a quantum experiment as a whole. We provide numerical and rigorous evidence that quantum experiments with low reactivity are simple by a host of measures: they are efficient to classically simulate, learn, and fast-forward. This motivates the search for quantum experiments with high reactivity, which may evade these simplistic features. To this end, we introduce easily implementable experimental protocols---dubbed Pauli path spectroscopy---that allow one to efficiently measure the reactivity of any quantum experiment of interest. Our protocols are applicable even when the experiment itself is beyond the reach of classical simulation.

</details>


### [68] [Characterising the precision of a clock without any external time reference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31832)
*Pablo Á. Domínguez,Christopher Chubb,Mischa P. Woods*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种仅用单个时钟自评估精度的方法，无需外部更优时间参考；通过信号分路器与延迟触发实现，估计量只依赖计数统计，并用电子电路实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上测量时钟精度须与更优时间参考比较，但物理上并不存在理想参考时钟，因此需要一种无需外部参考即可自评估时钟精度的手段。

Method: 仅使用一个时钟，配合信号分路器（signal splitter）与延迟触发（delayed trigger），并显式计入延迟触发自身的不确定度；由此构造的估计量只依赖时钟的滴答计数统计（tick-count statistics）。

Result: 用专门设计的电子电路进行验证，在无外部授时源、也无第二个时钟的条件下成功实现了精度自评估，证明了方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 单时钟可在仅借助分路与延迟触发的条件下刻画自身精度，为缺乏理想参考时的时钟计量提供了一种自校准式方案。

Abstract: Determining a clock's precision usually requires comparison with a better time reference, yet no ideal reference is physically attainable. We show how a single clock can characterise its own precision using only a signal splitter and delayed trigger, whose uncertainty is explicitly accounted for. The resulting estimator depends only on tick-count statistics. A purpose-built electronic circuit validates the method without an external timekeeper or a second clock.

</details>


### [69] [Measuring Clock Precision Without an Ideal Time Reference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31837)
*Pablo Á. Domínguez,Christopher Chubb,Mischa P. Woods*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究在无绝对时间参考的条件下，能否利用多个不完美时钟的集体行为确定其精度，并给出解析界与协议，同时将方法扩展到亚周期间隔与相对论效应测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理器件通常通过与更精确的参考标准比对来表征，但时钟缺乏物理上完美的时间参考，实际时钟具有有限的、依赖模型的不确定性，因此传统比对方法失效。作者由此提出核心问题：多个不完美时钟能否在不依赖绝对参考的情况下共同确定自身精度。

Method: 仅使用同一时钟的多个副本进行精度量化，副本之间无需同步，且允许存在制造差异或环境耦合；通过解析推导给出精度量化的理论上下界，并设计相应协议。

Result: 获得了刻画时钟精度的解析界，并提出了可在非同步、含制造变异与环境耦合条件下运行的协议；同时表明这些方法可用于测量亚周期时间间隔以及时间膨胀、多普勒频移等相对论效应。

Conclusion: 无需绝对参考，仅凭多个不完美时钟的集体比较即可定量刻画时钟精度，为量子计量学与基础物理研究提供了新的工具与测量手段。

Abstract: Characterising physical devices typically requires comparison with more precise reference standards. For clocks, however, this approach is problematic because no physically perfect time reference exists; real clocks have finite, model-dependent uncertainty. We address whether multiple imperfect clocks can collectively determine their precision without an absolute reference. This paper provides analytic bounds and protocols for quantifying clock precision using only multiple copies of the same clock, which need not be synchronised and may have manufacturing variations or environmental coupling. Beyond clock characterisation, these methods enable precise measurements f sub-period intervals and relativistic effects such as time dilation and Doppler shifts, providing tools for quantum metrology and fundamental physics.

</details>

Abstract: Quantum processes can exhibit indefinite causal order, allowing coherent superpositions of distinct orderings of events. Abstract: We report the first quantum-hardware implementation of Real-time Estimators for Scattering Observables (RESOs). Abstract: Selecting a data encoding is a central and poorly tooled decision in quantum machine learning. The feature map fixes the geometry of the Hilbert space, the expressibility of quantum kernels, and whether the circuit can run on near-term hardware. This paper presents Quantum Encoding Agents, an open-source system that turns encoding selection into a natural-language interaction. Given a dataset and an optional task description, it profiles the data, applies a hardware-aware policy using the gate-error threshold p* approximately 10^{-3}, returns a copyable Qiskit circuit with a justification in Portuguese or English, and scores the quantum kernel by Kernel-Target Alignment (KTA). When a numerical matrix is available, it estimates the correlation fractal dimension D_2 and selects original columns with FD-ASE, using q* = max(2, ceil(D_2)) as a qubit budget so that angle and IQP maps are not proposed at a width where the fidelity kernel has collapsed. The service implements seven encoding families: amplitude, angle, dense angle, IQP, basis, data re-uploading, and custom feature map. On benchmark datasets, KTA separates these encodings under realistic NISQ constraints.

</details>

Abstract: Ensemble nitrogen-vacancy (NV) centers are widely used for quantum sensing and magnetometry, where performance depends on the accurate implementation of microwave pulse sequences. In these systems, inhomogeneous broadening and short coherence times limit reliable multi-pulse control, while hardware imperfections and off-resonant excitation distort implemented rotations, making direct calibration of arbitrary gates tedious. To address this, we implement a control framework with a ZXZXZ gate decomposition which uses fixed-angle, phase-parameterized pulses, enabling universal single-qubit control from a single calibrated $ π/2$ primitive ($X_{π/2}$) along with virtual Z operations. We compare a standard pulse with a composite pulse designed for high-power operation in a regime where the Rabi frequency is comparable to the hyperfine splitting, leading to non-uniform evolution across hyperfine levels. Using pre-determined combinations of $π/2$ pulse sequences, we observe that standard pulses rapidly deviate from ideal behavior under repeated application, while the composite pulse suppresses this error accumulation by more than 70$\%$ for longer sequences despite its longer duration. In contrast, randomized benchmarking yields similar decay factors for both implementations, highlighting its limited sensitivity to coherent control errors. These results establish a practical route to robust control in short-coherence ensemble systems and emphasize the importance of pulse-level characterization.

</details>


### [76] [Quantum error mitigation from information dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31934)

Main category: quant-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模复杂量子电路中，量子信息沿指数级多的路径扩散，使得直接理解误差影响极为困难，实验误差成为量子科学的核心挑战，因此亟需新的误差理解与缓解框架。

Method: 提出量子电路信息动力学的紧凑表示——反应性函数（reactivity function），其形状决定电路的噪声响应及对不同误差缓解方法的适用性；基于此设计两种互补方法：一是Pauli-path零噪声外推（拟合参数化模型到反应性函数以精确外推到零噪声），二是可调误差消除（滤波反应性函数以抵消阈值局域性以内的噪声）；两者均含调优参数，可通过系统调优提升精度直至收敛。

Result: 在来自已发表实验的三个电路族上，所提方法克服了传统零噪声外推高达30%的偏差；在约10^-2均方根误差水平下，相比概率误差消除（PEC）将采样成本最多降低约10^3倍。

Conclusion: 反应性函数为理解与缓解量子电路误差提供了统一且紧凑的视角，由此导出的两种方法具有可系统调优、高精度、低采样成本的优势，能有效克服传统方法的偏差。

Abstract: Overcoming experimental errors is a central challenge in quantum science. This challenge is particularly acute in large and complex quantum circuits, where quantum information spreads into exponentially many paths, foiling any direct attempt to understand errors' impact. In this work, we present a novel approach to understanding and mitigating experimental errors, based on a surprisingly compact representation of a quantum circuit's information dynamics, the reactivity function. The shape of a circuit's reactivity function governs its noise response, and its amenability to different error mitigation methods. From this viewpoint, we introduce two complementary such methods. The first, Pauli-path zero-noise extrapolation, fits a parameterized model to a circuit's reactivity function and uses it to accurately extrapolate to zero noise. The second, tunable error cancellation, filters a circuit's reactivity function to cancel noise on all information up to a threshold locality. Crucially, both methods feature a tuning parameter that allows one to systematically improve their accuracy until convergence. Across three circuit families from published experiments, our methods overcome conventional zero-noise extrapolation biases as large as 30%; at $\sim$$10^{-2}$ root-mean-square error, they reduce sampling cost relative to probabilistic error cancellation by up to a factor of $10^3$.

</details>


### [77] [Learning Trotter Orderings for Heisenberg Hamiltonians with a Ranking Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.31977)
*Shamminuj Aktar,Reuben Tate,Stephan Eidenbenz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出物理感知排序Transformer，直接学习Trotter化中哈密顿量项的最优排序，单次前向推理即可生成顺序，并在链、三角与矩形晶格上泛化到更大规模系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Trotter化通过顺序应用哈密顿量各项近似量子时间演化，但非对易项会引入与顺序相关的误差，最优项排序是阶乘级搜索空间上的组合优化问题。既有方法依赖固定启发式或在预定义结构化顺序中挑选，均需先仿真候选再决策，代价高且难以扩展。

Conclusion: 学习式排序可替代启发式与候选仿真搜索，为Trotter化的项排序提供高效且可泛化的方案；依赖对易子结构建模与排序损失设计，且在链与三角晶格上泛化最优，为更大规模量子模拟的顺序优化提供可行路径。

Abstract: Trotterization approximates quantum time evolution by sequentially applying Hamiltonian terms. Because noncommuting terms introduce ordering-dependent errors, selecting an optimal term ordering is a combinatorial problem over a factorial search space. Prior approaches rely on fixed heuristics or on selection among predefined structured orderings, both of which require simulating candidates before choosing among them. For 1D and 2D Heisenberg-style Hamiltonians, we instead learn the ordering directly with a physics-aware ranking transformer that assigns a scalar score to each Hamiltonian term and predicts an ordering by sorting these scores. Physical structure enters through commutator-biased attention and an anticommutation-weighted ranking loss, and the models are trained on simulated-annealing (SA) reference orderings. We train separate models for first- and second-order Trotterization, each jointly on chains up to 14 qubits and lattices up to 12 qubits, and evaluate them on unseen chains with 16-20 qubits and lattices with 16 and 20 qubits. Predicted orderings are evaluated by the median gap in simulation fidelity to the SA reference. At first order, the model reaches a pooled gap below 10^-4 on chains, 0.0144 and 0.0088 on triangular lattices, and 0.0948 and 0.0823 on rectangular lattices at 16 and 20 qubits; at second order, the gaps are 0.0114, 0.0364 and 0.0244, and 0.1348 and 0.1239. The prediction exceeds the SA reference on 34% of first-order and 8% of second-order chain instances and can match its fidelity even when the two sequences differ because commuting terms may be rearranged without changing the Trotter unitary. The learned models generalize to larger systems across all three geometries, performing best on chains and triangular lattices, and produce an ordering in one forward pass without candidate enumeration, simulated annealing, or fidelity evaluation.

</details>


### [78] [Robust Entanglement Witnessing via Dense Network Coding with Graph States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32024)
*Brian Doolittle,Ian George*

TL;DR: 本文研究含噪环境下量子密集网络编码（dense network coding）的纠缠见证问题，给出经典网络成功概率上界，并证明噪声鲁棒性随发送方数量指数增强，提出半设备无关协议以稳健估计图态与测量基的有效可见度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际通信网络中应用基于纠缠的方案，需要能在噪声存在时验证纠缠的实用测试方法；此外希望减少对状态制备与测量设备的表征假设（器件无关性）。

Method: 考虑利用图态纠缠实现一族仿射变换的密集网络编码协议；推导经典网络可达成功概率的界；分析噪声鲁棒性随发送方数量的缩放；并据此构造半设备无关协议，用于稳健估计图态与测量基的有效可见度。

Overall, our approaches are robust to noise, can support the usage of uncharacterized state preparations or measurements, and can be applied to any graph state.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 相互作用对时间晶体序的保护或破坏取决于驱动类型，toggling-frame图像解释了多极驱动下的退相干，而相同zJ下网格优于环状表明存在超越该图像的协调性效应；所有比值在三种相干时间定义下均成立。

Abstract: We show that stronger interactions protect periodically driven time-crystal order against dephasing but destabilise order under random multipolar drives, and that coordination matters beyond the total interaction strength.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: The entangling layer of a quantum circuit Born machine can supply both entanglement and nonstabilizerness. In this work we show that the entangling layer can be a Clifford circuit, which supplies no nonstabilizerness. We use an architecture in which a fixed, non-trainable entangler is reused in every layer and only single-qubit rotations are optimized. There, a Clifford entangler reaches the same Kullback--Leibler divergence as a near-Haar one at sufficient circuit depth, and entanglers spanning zero to Haar-typical nonstabilizerness reach the same loss, up to a small offset for a free-fermion matchgate entangler. A doping control that raises the entangler's nonstabilizerness while leaving the entangling power of every gate unchanged leaves the loss flat. With a Clifford entangler the nonstabilizerness of the trained state comes entirely from the rotations, which take the trained circuit outside the stabilizer formalism. We also show that, among all pure states whose Born distribution is a given target, the one whose amplitudes are the positive square roots of the probabilities has the smallest stabilizer Rényi entropy, so this minimum can be computed without training.

</details>


### [81] [Swapping Quantum Annealing Errors into a Cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33975)
*Hao Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出腔量子退火方案：在退火中段耦合频率 O(1)≫Δ 的腔模，将小能隙处产生的错误转换为光子，从而缩短退火时间并实现结果认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子退火在小能隙 Δ 处速度变慢，且该处产生的错误无法被检测，限制了其保真度与效率。

Method: 引入腔量子退火：将频率为 O(1)（远大于 Δ）的腔模在退火中途耦合进系统，把退火错误交换为光子；通过计数光子对结果进行认证。反向运行该交换（预置光子）可制备激发态。

Result: 在 p-spin 模型中，退火时间缩短至无腔情形的近平方根；高光子运行的产率为 O(1)，其基态保真度超过 99.99%，且所需时间数量级更短，该增益随系统规模指数增长。

Conclusion: 腔模可增强基于量子比特的计算，为量子退火实现高保真量子态制备开辟了道路。

Abstract: Quantum annealing is a promising form of quantum computation, but it slows down at a small energy gap $Δ$ during the anneal, and the errors it makes there remain undetected. We introduce cavity quantum annealing, in which a cavity mode of frequency $O(1)\ggΔ$, coupled mid-schedule, swaps these errors into photons. In the $p$-spin model, this reduces the annealing time to nearly the square root of that required without the cavity. Moreover, counting the photons certifies the result: high-photon runs, with an $O(1)$ yield, reach a ground-state fidelity above $99.99\%$ orders of magnitude sooner, a gain that grows exponentially with system size. A pre-loaded photon instead runs the swap in reverse, preparing an excited state. More broadly, our work shows that cavities can enhance computations performed with qubits, paving the way toward high-fidelity quantum state preparation by quantum annealing.

</details>


### [82] [Particles, Localization and "Collapse"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32101)
*Anthony Rizzi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Hegerfeldt定理表明，在量子力学与狭义相对论的相当一般假设下，粒子（或场）的定域化是不可能的。本文面向本科生，运用量子力学的系综诠释（最小诠释），澄清量子力学与Hegerfeldt定理的基本原理与前提，以解决该定理引发的定域性、坍缩假设与局域测量等根本性困惑，并给出一个由坍缩假设导致错误结果的新例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hegerfeldt定理及其相关结果对量子理论的理解提出尖锐挑战：坍缩假设的使用受到质疑，粒子乃至定域场的存在受到质疑，局域测量也因此被挑战。这些问题困扰研究者与学生，可能损害学生学习该内容的能力甚至对物理的兴趣，使量子场论及其量子力学极限显得难以理解甚至不自洽。作者因此需要一个对本科生可及的解决方案。

Method: 采用量子力学的自然（最小）诠释，即系综诠释；阐释并澄清量子力学、Hegerfeldt定理及其在测量中应用所依据的基本原理与前提。

Result: 给出了一个由坍缩假设所导致错误结果的、性质上新的例子；通过系综诠释化解了Hegerfeldt定理带来的相关困难。

Conclusion: 以对本科生可及的方式解决了Hegerfeldt定理引发的定域化、坍缩假设与局域测量等根本问题，有助于恢复学生对量子场论及其量子力学极限的可理解性与自洽性认识。

Abstract: Hegerfeldt's theorem shows that the localization of a particle (or field) is not possible under very general assumptions of quantum mechanics and special relativity. This theorem, and further developments and associated theorems force penetrating questions on the proper understanding of the theory and its use. In particular, the use of the collapse postulate is called into question. The existence of particles and even localized fields are called into question. And, this in turn challenges local measurements. It generally forces one to consider the profound problems and even wrong results that arise from the collapse postulate. We give a qualitatively new example of such a wrong result. Researchers and students alike are bothered by these fundamental issues. Students' ability to learn the material, and even their interest in physics, can be compromised by such disturbing conundra that seem to make physics, or at least QFT and its QM limit, seem incomprehensible and even inconsistent. This article resolves these issues in a way accessible to undergraduates, by applying the natural (minimal) interpretation of quantum mechanics (QM) (the ensemble interpretation) and explicating and clarifying the basic principles and premises used in QM and the Hegerfeldt theorem and its application to measurements.

</details>

*Alexis R. Legón,Pedro Orellana,Ariel Norambuena*

Main category: quant-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BIC为波导量子电动力学中保持纠缠提供了机制，但如何鲁棒地制备高保真度的纠缠BIC仍是关键问题。

Method: 将有效原子-波导耦合的准Floquet调制与独立相位控制的原子交换相结合，为制备完整缀饰BIC提供受限的反绝热辅助；并用有限模式微观模拟与静态校准误差扫描进行验证。

Result: 无损有效模型下，快速控制协议给出全态BIC保真度0.99992、无条件原子Bell保真度0.99588；有限模式模拟验证了通道过程中的中心-边带描述；弛豫与退相干研究识别出有限的可用纠缠窗口并显示耦合包络整形的益处。

Conclusion: 结果支持在给定的控制与噪声假设下，对缀饰纠缠进行受控制备、保持与读取。

Abstract: Bound states in the continuum (BICs) provide a mechanism for preserving entanglement in waveguide quantum electrodynamics. Here, using quantum control techniques in a configuration of two braided giant atoms, we propose a robust approach to create high-fidelity entangled BICs. The protocol combines quasi-Floquet modulation of the effective atom--waveguide couplings with an independent phase-controlled atomic exchange, which provides restricted counterdiabatic assistance for preparing the complete dressed BIC. In the lossless effective model, we obtain a full-state BIC fidelity of $0.99992$ and an unconditional atomic Bell fidelity of $0.99588$ using a fast control protocol. Microscopic finite-mode simulations validate the central-sideband description during the passage, while static calibration-error scans quantify its control tolerances. A separate microscopic study with relaxation and dephasing, using experimentally motivated component scales, identifies a finite useful entanglement window and a benefit from shaping the coupling envelopes. These results support controlled preparation, retention, and retrieval of dressed entanglement within the stated control and noise assumptions.

</details>


### [84] [Inverse-Designed Nanobeam Cavities for High-Cooperativity Spin-Photon Interfaces in Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32205)
*Nail Letty,Md Sakibul Islam,Wayesh Qarony*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用统一的伴随优化框架，为硅中T色心和Al1色心逆向设计纳米束腔，实现了高Purcell增强、定向波导耦合及纳米级制造扰动下的高协同度，首次给出Al1色心的腔设计方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信波段硅自旋缺陷可将可扩展硅光子学与自旋量子存储器、多量子比特寄存器结合，是集成量子网络的理想平台。T色心与Al1色心的零声子相干发射较弱，需强腔增强才能实现高效自旋-光子界面，因此亟需针对这两类色心的高性能腔设计。

Method: 采用统一的伴随优化（adjoint-optimization）框架，以Purcell增强、谐振对准和光子提取为目标，对两类色心分别设计硅纳米束腔；对称腔用于最大化Purcell增强，非对称腔用于向集成波导定向耦合；并用三维有限差分时域（FDTD）仿真进行评估。

Result: 仿真得到负载品质因子最高达3.25×10^6，Purcell因子最高1.43×10^5，协同度约280–5000，腔耦合发射比例超过99.6%；非对称腔可将约90%发射功率导入单一片上波导，同时协同度仍高于280。在纳米级制造扰动下四种设计均保持高协同度，且当腔品质因子被限制在吸收极限的10^5时高协同度依然保持。

Conclusion: 该统一且与制造兼容的框架为硅电信自旋-光子界面提供了可扩展的腔设计路径，并据作者所知是首个针对Al1色心的腔设计方案。

Abstract: Telecom-band spin defects in silicon offer a compelling platform for integrated quantum networks by combining scalable silicon photonics with spin-based quantum memories and multiqubit registers. The T center and recently demonstrated Al1 center are particularly promising, but their limited coherent zero-phonon emission requires strong cavity enhancement for efficient spin-photon interfaces. Here, we inverse-design silicon nanobeam cavities for both centers using a unified adjoint-optimization framework targeting Purcell enhancement, resonance alignment, and photon extraction. For each emitter, symmetric cavities maximize Purcell enhancement, while asymmetric cavities provide directional coupling to an integrated waveguide. Three-dimensional finite-difference time-domain simulations yield loaded quality factors up to 3.25 x 10^6, Purcell factors up to 1.43 x 10^5, and cooperativities of approximately 280-5000, corresponding to cavity-coupled emission fractions exceeding 99.6%. The asymmetric cavities direct approximately 90% of emitted power into a single on-chip waveguide while maintaining cooperativity above 280. Under nanometer-scale fabrication perturbations, all four designs remain within the high-cooperativity regime, and high cooperativity persists when the cavity quality factor is constrained to a realistic absorption-limited value of 10^5. This unified, fabrication-compatible framework establishes a scalable cavity-design route for silicon telecom spin-photon interfaces and, to our knowledge, the first cavity designs for the Al1 center.

</details>


### [85] [Contour-integral and Fourier transform based multivariable quantum eigenvalue transformation for commuting matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32262)
*Shan Jiang,Dong An*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在量子计算机上实现多变量矩阵值函数，提出两种针对一组两两交换矩阵的多变量矩阵特征值变换量子算法：一种基于多变量围道积分（适用于 $\mathbb{C}^n$ 上任意全纯函数），另一种基于高维傅里叶变换（适用于交换 Hermitian 矩阵的光滑函数），并分析其复杂度及混合量子-经典变体，最后给出多变量矩阵多项式的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子算法多针对单变量或较受限的函数变换，缺乏对多变量矩阵值函数的通用实现框架；同时多变量情形下辅助量子比特数与查询复杂度之间的权衡尚不明确，需要系统研究以支撑更广泛的应用。

Method: 提出两种算法：1）基于多变量围道积分，处理 $\mathbb{C}^n$ 上的任意全纯函数；2）基于高维傅里叶变换，面向交换 Hermitian 矩阵的光滑函数。两者均借助量子奇异值变换、压缩小工具（compression gadgets）与酉算子线性组合进行量子实现与复杂度分析，并进一步研究混合量子-经典变体。

Result: 两种算法均能实现作用于两两交换矩阵元组的多变量矩阵特征值变换；混合量子-经典变体可将所需辅助比特数降至对变量数的对数依赖，代价是查询复杂度增加；作为应用，多变量矩阵多项式可在不显式依赖次数的情况下实现，仅取决于多项式的全局性质。

Conclusion: 该工作为多变量矩阵值函数的量子实现提供了通用且复杂度可控的框架，揭示了辅助比特数与查询复杂度之间的权衡关系，并扩展了矩阵多项式在量子计算中的实现能力。

Abstract: We study the problem of implementing multivariable matrix-valued functions on a quantum computer and propose two quantum algorithms for multivariable matrix eigenvalue transformations acting on tuples of pairwise commuting matrices. The first algorithm is based on multivariable contour integrals and applies to arbitrary holomorphic functions on $\mathbb{C}^n$. The second algorithm is based on high-dimensional Fourier transforms and designed for smooth functions of commuting Hermitian matrices. For both algorithms, we discuss and analyze the complexity of their quantum implementation leveraging quantum singular value transformation, compression gadgets, and linear combination of unitaries. We additionally study their hybrid quantum--classical variants that reduce the number of required ancilla qubits to logarithmic dependence on the number of variables, at the cost of increased query complexity. As an application, we show that multivariable matrix polynomials can be implemented with no additional explicit degree dependence, depending only on global properties of the polynomials.

</details>


### [86] [Temperature--Hamiltonian Ambiguity in Strong-Coupling Quantum Equilibrium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33389)
*Jinshuang Jin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"温度—哈密顿量歧义"问题：强耦合下约化平衡态只固定无量纲Gibbs生成元，无法唯一确定局域哈密顿量与温度；作者通过独立物理信息识别局域哈密顿量，给出可操作消歧框架，并用两个精确可解模型验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合使子系统偏离裸哈密顿量的Gibbs态，局域温度与局域哈密顿量的定义变得不唯一。传统上从约化态反推Gibbs表示时，温度与哈密顿量之间存在参数化冗余，需要区分"状态表示"与"热力学指派"，为弱耦合之外的局域热力学提供操作基础。

Method: 提出将局域哈密顿量由与稳态Gibbs参数化无关的物理信息（如滞后/推迟动力学确定的重整化激发尺度）独立识别；在精确约化态与该哈密顿量生成的Gibbs族相容时，唯一定出局域温度。用可精确求解的两能级-谐振子模型（温度重整化 vs 平均力哈密顿量能级重整化的状态等价描述）和精确玻色子模型进行对照分析。

Result: 在两能级-谐振子模型中显式展示了温度重整化与平均力哈密顿量能级重整化给出的状态等价描述，从而暴露歧义；在精确玻色子模型中，推迟动力学独立固定重整化局域激发尺度，从而在由稳态布居确定温度之前先确定局域哈密顿量，给出互补消歧路径。

Conclusion: 温度—哈密顿量歧义可通过独立物理信息操作性地消解；该框架区分状态表示与热力学指派，为强耦合下的局域热力学提供可操作定义。局域温度的确定依赖于局域哈密顿量的独立识别，而非仅由约化态决定。

Abstract: Strong coupling generally drives a subsystem away from the Gibbs state of its bare Hamiltonian at the bath temperature. We formulate a temperature--Hamiltonian ambiguity: the reduced equilibrium state fixes the dimensionless Gibbs generator, but does not by itself uniquely determine the local Hamiltonian and temperature entering a Gibbs representation. We propose an operational resolution in which the local Hamiltonian is identified from physical information independent of the stationary Gibbs parametrization. Whenever the exact reduced state is compatible with the Gibbs family generated by that independently identified Hamiltonian, it then determines a unique local temperature. An exactly solvable two-level-system--oscillator model makes the ambiguity explicit through state-equivalent descriptions based on temperature renormalization relative to a specified local Hamiltonian and Hamiltonian-of-mean-force level renormalization at the bath temperature. An exact bosonic model provides the complementary case in which retarded dynamics independently fixes a renormalized local excitation scale and thereby specifies the local Hamiltonian before the stationary populations determine the temperature. These results distinguish state representation from thermodynamic assignment and provide an operational framework for local thermodynamics beyond weak coupling.

</details>


### [87] [Conserved and relaxing modes enable polynomial-success coherent Carleman lattice Boltzmann evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32310)
*Hua-Dong Yao,Sauro Succi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于加权Hermite矩基与MRT碰撞算子的量子Carleman格子Boltzmann方案，通过分块编码实现近单位单步成功概率，使总开销随步数多项式增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Carleman线性化的量子块编码在布居空间中进行，后选择概率低，导致相干多步演化的整体成功概率随步数指数衰减，难以实用。

Method: 在加权Hermite矩基下采用多松弛时间(MRT)碰撞算子进行Carleman线性化；利用提升后的碰撞算子分解为小耦合块（守恒模与松弛模耦合导致块范数超过1），构造范数达到型的块编码；并按给定演化视界缩放"对偶扇区"以控制编码与扇区选择开销。

Result: 单步成功概率接近1；编码与扇区选择总开销随步数多项式增长而非指数增长；百步演化总成功概率比布居空间编码高数个数量级；几十步视界内截断动力学复现基准流动的非线性生成模态，误差在百分之几以内；给出完整门级资源估计并验证制备-演化-读出为单一酉电路。

Conclusion: 守恒模与松弛模的分块结构在三维格点上同样成立，为具有二次平衡态的格子Boltzmann模型提供了构造范数达到型编码的可扩展路径。

Abstract: Carleman linearization represents nonlinear lattice Boltzmann collision dynamics in a form amenable to quantum block encoding, but low postselection probabilities in existing population-space encodings make coherent multi-step evolution exponentially unlikely to succeed. We present a quantum Carleman lattice Boltzmann scheme based on the multi-relaxation-time collision operator in a weighted Hermite moment basis. In this basis, the lifted collision operator separates into small coupled blocks whose norm exceeds unity because of the coupling between conserved and relaxing modes. This block structure enables a norm-attaining block encoding with a single-step success probability close to unity. Crucially, scaling the pair sector for a prescribed evolution horizon makes the combined encoding and sector-selection overhead grow polynomially rather than exponentially with the number of steps. For a hundred-step evolution, the resulting complete-run success probability exceeds that of population-space encodings by orders of magnitude. Over horizons of several tens of steps, the truncated dynamics reproduces the nonlinearly generated modes of a benchmark flow to within a few percent. We verify the combined preparation, evolution and readout protocol as a single unitary circuit and give a complete gate-level resource estimate for extracting a nonlinear observable of the benchmark flow from a coherent Carleman evolution. The block structure of conserved and relaxing modes holds equally on three-dimensional lattices and provides a constructive route to norm-attaining encodings for lattice Boltzmann models with quadratic equilibria.

</details>


### [88] [Exact High-Temperature Quantum Area Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32329)
*Ahmad Yousefi,Ali T. Rezakhani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明了局部晶格哈密顿量热态的量子互信息满足 β² 面积律：当逆温 β < β* 时，I_β(A,B) ≤ f̃(β)β²|∂_{AB}|，且 f̃(β) 在 β→0 时保持有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有线性面积律只给出与温度无关的宽松上界，无法刻画高温下相关性随温度消失的速率，也难以与宏观热力学的退耦合行为（可提取功、界面束缚能随 β 趋于零）相匹配，因此需要更精确的温度标度。

Method: 针对局部晶格哈密顿量的热态，对任意二分割 A|B，在临界逆温 β* 以下分析量子互信息 I_β(A,B) 的边界依赖与温度依赖，证明其被 f̃(β)β²|∂_{AB}| 控制，并考察 β→0 极限下 f̃(β) 的有限性。

Result: 得到 I_β(A,B) ≤ f̃(β)β²|∂_{AB}|，即互信息按 β² 标度、相关函数按 β 线性标度；由此推出退耦合后的非平衡自由能与界面总能量均正比于界面面积 |∂_{AB}|，并在高温下（β→0）随 β 消失。

Conclusion: β² 面积律改进了高温相关性与宏观热力学之间的联系，使高温互信息与相关按逆温的二次、线性方式被抑制，与宏观热力学量预期的热退耦合一致。

Abstract: We prove a $β^{2}$ area law for the quantum mutual information of thermal states of local lattice Hamiltonians: for any bipartitioning $A|B$ and all inverse temperatures $β$ below a critical value $β^{*}$, we show $\mathcal{I}_β(A,B) \leqslant \tilde{f}(β)\, β^{2}\,|\partial_{AB}|$, where $|\partial_{AB}|$ denotes the size of the boundary region and $\tilde{f}(β)$ remains finite as $β\to 0$. This quadratic scaling improves the relationship between high-temperature correlations and macroscopic thermodynamics. While a linear area law implicitly permits a loose, temperature-independent upper bound on extractable work and binding energy, the quadratic scaling ensures that the nonequilibrium free energy available after decoupling regions and the total energy of the interface both are proportional to the area of the interface of $A|B$ and vanish as $β$ at high temperatures. These results show that the high-temperature mutual information and correlation are suppressed, respectively, quadratically and linearly in inverse temperature, which are consistent with the expected thermal decoupling in macroscopic thermodynamic quantities.

</details>


### [89] [Correlation measure for statistical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34859)
*V. I. Yukalov,E. P. Yukalova*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出"关联指数"这一通用关联度量，适用于量子与经典、平衡与非平衡统计系统，并以多种密度算符与约化密度矩阵为例说明其计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计系统中需要一个更通用的关联度量，能够同时适用于量子与经典、平衡与非平衡系统。

Method: 对任意迹类算符定义关联指数的一般概念，并通过密度算符和约化密度矩阵的实例展示计算；平衡情形下以玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚玻色系统、超导系统与自旋系统为例，非平衡情形下考虑自旋与赝自旋系统的非线性动力学。

Result: 给出了关联指数的一般定义，并在平衡与非平衡多种物理系统中完成了具体计算示例。

Conclusion: 关联指数是一种具有广泛适用性的统计系统关联度量，可用于量子与经典、平衡与非平衡系统的关联分析。

Abstract: A measure of correlations for statistical systems is advanced, called correlation index. The measure is general, being applicable to quantum and classical, equilibrium and nonequilibrium statistical systems. After formulating the general notion of correlation indices for arbitrary trace-class operators, the calculation of the correlation indices are illustrated by several examples of density operators and reduced density matrices. The computation of the correlation indices for equilibrium systems is exemplified for bosonic systems with the Bose-Einstein condensate, superconducting systems, and for spin systems. Correlation indices for nonequilibrium systems are considered for nonlinear dynamics of spin and pseudospin systems.

</details>


### [90] [Autonomous phase discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33802)
*Shiyu Zhou,Yuxuan Zhang,Sebastian Wetzel,Roger Melko,Xiu-Zhe Luo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出结合可微编程与无监督学习的自主系统，通过自适应轨迹探索哈密顿量空间，实现无需相标签或预设目标的量子相发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子相研究依赖对称性、拓扑等直觉与数学工具，但缺乏在组织原理未知的哈密顿量空间中通用探索的方法。

Method: 将可微编程与无监督学习结合，沿自适应轨迹评估基态数据而非固定参数网格；用三种求解器实现，并在相同基态评估预算下与随机采样对比基准测试。

Result: 在含多达200种不同相的广义团簇链上，相同预算下多找到至多25种相，并以30倍预算匹配随机采样；在50参数陈绝缘体上到达简谐构造未获得的分区，同时复现采样找到的全部分区。

Conclusion: 建立了梯度驱动的哈密顿量空间自主探索范式，为无需相标签或指定目标相的量子相发现提供实用路径。

Abstract: Understanding quantum phases of matter has long relied on physicists' intuition and mathematical tools such as symmetry and topology. Remarkably successful as these approaches have been, they provide no universal way to explore a Hamiltonian space whose organizing principle is not known in advance. In this work, we introduce a fully autonomous system combining differentiable programming and unsupervised learning for quantum phase discovery. The search evaluates ground-state data along an adaptive trajectory rather than on a predetermined parameter grid. We demonstrate the system with three different solvers and benchmark it against random sampling at equal ground-state-evaluation budgets. On a generalized cluster chain hosting up to $200$ distinct phases, the search finds up to $25$ more phases at the same budget, and matches random sampling given thirty times its budget. On a $50$-parameter Chern insulator, it reaches sectors not obtained by the simple harmonic constructions considered here, in a family whose inverse problem remains open, while recovering all sectors found by sampling. Our results establish autonomous, gradient-driven exploration of Hamiltonian space as a practical route to discovering quantum phases without phase labels or a prescribed target phase.

</details>


### [91] [A Quantum Framework for K Coloring of Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32348)
*Lord Sen,Shyamapada Mukherjee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一个求解器无关的量子图着色框架，覆盖精确着色（Grover搜索）与近似最优着色（QAOA、量子退火），将量子比特需求从SOTA的O(NK)降至O(N log2 K)，并配合优化的比较器电路强制邻接约束，可选对称性图约简预处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图着色是经典NP完全问题，在调度、寄存器分配、频率分配和网络优化中有广泛应用。量子计算在特定实例上具备多项式甚至超多项式加速潜力，但实际量子图着色必须权衡量子比特资源、电路深度与约束强制，因此需要更高效的编码与约束实现。

Method: 面向K着色提出求解器无关的量子框架：设计新颖编码与高效约束实现；精确着色采用Grover搜索，近似最优着色采用QAOA与量子退火；用量子比特从O(NK)降至O(N log2 K)，并采用优化的比较器电路强制邻接约束；此外加入基于对称性的图约简作为可选预处理步骤，在量子执行前缩小实例规模。

Result: 相对SOTA的O(NK)量子比特用量，本框架将量子比特需求降至O(N log2 K)，并通过优化比较器电路实现邻接约束的高效编码；对称性图约简作为可选预处理可进一步降低执行前的实例规模。

Conclusion: 该求解器无关框架在保持精确与近似着色能力的同时显著减少量子比特开销，提升资源效率，并可结合图约简预处理进一步适配当前量子硬件约束。

Abstract: Graph coloring is a well-known NP-complete problem with applications in scheduling, register allocation, frequency assignment, and network optimization. Quantum computing offers the potential for polynomial or even super-polynomial speedups in certain problem instances, yet practical quantum graph coloring methods must carefully balance qubit resources, circuit depth, and constraint enforcement. We proposed a solver agnostic quantum framework for $K$-coloring. Along with a novel encoding and efficient constraint implementation for its exact coloring exploiting Grover search and almost optimal coloring by the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm \emph{(QAOA)}, and \emph{Quantum Annealing}. Unlike using $O(NK)$ qubits as in \emph{SOTA}, we reduced the qubit requirements to $O(N \log_2 K)$, along with optimized comparator circuits enforcing adjacency constraints. Further symmetry-based graph reduction is incorporated as an optional preprocessing step to further reduce the instance size before quantum execution.

</details>


### [92] [Optimal performance in a chaotic Floquet quantum battery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35655)
*Lakshmija N K,Uma Divakaran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究N量子比特均匀相互作用的量子电池，通过周期性横向踢加相互作用方向无序充电，无序使系统离开置换对称子空间从而储存更多能量；大无序极限下充电呈混沌，储能可用随机矩阵理论精确计算，能量涨落趋于可忽略，且退相干存储阶段遍历性接近储能，效率接近1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池的最佳运行需要同时实现最大储能与最小能量涨落，而均匀相互作用的量子比特电池通常受置换对称性限制，储能能力受限。作者希望利用无序打破置换对称子空间的动力学约束，探索无序对储能、涨落及可提取性的影响，以寻找更优的充电方案。

Method: 考虑N个均匀相互作用的量子比特电池，从基态出发，在横向施加周期性kick，同时在相互作用方向引入无序进行充电。分析动力学是否保持在置换对称子空间；在大无序极限下借助随机矩阵理论计算储能。随后引入退相干噪声描述存储阶段，并计算遍历性（ergotropy）以评估能量可提取性。

Result: 无序使充电动力学离开置换对称子空间，相比无无序情形储存更多能量。大无序极限下出现混沌充电相，储能可由随机矩阵理论精确计算。能量涨落随无序增大而减小，在大无序极限下可忽略，同时储能达到最大。大无序下遍历性接近储能，表明能量几乎可完全提取，效率趋近于1。

Conclusion: 无序是提升量子电池性能的有效资源：它打破置换对称性约束，增加储能，抑制能量涨落，并使可提取能量（遍历性）接近总储能，从而实现高效量子电池。该工作为无序辅助量子电池的优化设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study a quantum battery consisting of $N$ qubits interacting uniformly with each other. Starting from its ground state, the battery is charged by introducing periodic kicks in the transverse direction along with adding disorder in the interaction direction. Although the battery Hamiltonian along with the initial state preserves the permutation symmetry, the dynamics in presence of disorder during the charging phase takes the system out of the permutation symmetric subspace, resulting to a larger amount of stored energy as compared to that without disorder. In the limit of large disorder, this interplay brings in a possibility of a chaotic charging phase when stored energy can be exactly calculated using random matrix theory. More importantly, we also find that the energy fluctuations decrease and become negligible in the limit of large disorder, which is also when the energy stored is maximum, a condition highly desirable for the optimum functioning of the battery. We complete the battery cycle by considering the storage phase in the presence of dephasing noise. We find that for larger disorder strengths, the ergotropy remains close to the stored energy, indicating nearly complete energy extractability with an efficiency approaching unity.

</details>


### [93] [In-plane optimal qubit operation with control fidelities exceeding 99 %](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33229)
*Yu-Chen Zhou,Zhenzhen Kong,Rong-Long Ma,Hao-Tian Jiang,Ao-Ran Li,Yang Zhong,Zhi-Tao Wu,Rui Zheng,Gui-Lei Wang,Gang Cao,Hai-Ou Li,Guo-Ping Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在平面锗量子点中实现单空穴自旋量子比特，通过关联自旋-电 susceptiblity 与相干性/控制性能，找到噪声最优工作点，使 Hahn-echo 相干时间提升5倍、控制错误率降低近10倍，最大门保真度达99.82%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空穴自旋量子比特因全电操控适合规模化量子计算，但价带空穴的多种物理机制导致比特性质各向异性。作者希望延长相干时间并获得高保真操控。

Method: 在平面锗量子点中制备单空穴自旋量子比特，研究面内磁场下其对电荷噪声的各向异性敏感度；将纵向自旋-电 susceptiblity 与相干性及控制性能关联，识别电荷噪声敏感度最小的工作点；并通过优化磁场方向来降低 susceptibility，用 Hann-echo 和门集层析评估。

Result: 优化磁场方向后自旋-电 susceptiblity 降低，Hahn-echo 相干时间提升5倍，控制错误率被抑制近10倍；在最优工作点，门集层析显示最大门保真度为99.82%。

Conclusion: 该发现提升了空穴自旋量子比特用于可扩展量子信息处理的前景。

Abstract: Hole spin qubits based on semiconductor quantum dots are promising for building future large-scale quantum computers owing to their all-electrical manipulation. However, abundant physical mechanisms of valence band holes lead to anisotropic qubit properties. There is an opportunity to prolong the coherence time and achieve high-fidelity qubit manipulations. Here, we report a single-hole spin qubit in a planar germanium quantum dot and investigate its anisotropic susceptibility to charge noise under an in-plane magnetic field. By correlating the longitudinal spin-electric susceptibility with qubit coherence and control performance, we identify an optimal operating point where the sensitivity to charge noise is minimized. We find that optimizing the magnetic-field orientation reduces the spin-electric susceptibility, resulting in a five-fold enhancement of the Hahn-echo coherence time and a nearly tenfold suppression of control infidelity. At the optimal operating point, gate set tomography demonstrates the maximum gate fidelity of 99.82 %. Our finding enhances the prospects of hole spin qubits for scalable quantum information processing.

</details>


### [94] [Feedback-based quantum optimization with low depth and measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32433)
*Zi-Wen Huang,Jia-Cheng Fan,Xiao-Hui Ni,Su-Juan Qin,Xiao-Kai Hou,Wei Huang,Bing-Jie Xu,Fei Gao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 BLS-FALQON，在保持与 SO-FALQON 相当电路深度的同时减少测量开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FALQON 求解组合优化问题无需经典参数优化，但需要深量子电路；SO-FALQON 虽深度降低最多，却带来每步 2.3 倍的测量开销。

Method: 受回溯线搜索（BLS）理论启发，提出 BLS-FALQON，用更少测量确定控制系数以降低电路深度。

Result: 在 8–20 顶点 max-cut 数值模拟中，相比 SO-FALQON 总测量次数减少 37.7%，电路深度相当；并在基于祖冲之二号超导处理器的天衍-176 量子计算机上验证有效。

Conclusion: BLS-FALQON 在真实量子硬件条件下兼顾低电路深度与低测量开销，优于 SO-FALQON。

Abstract: Feedback-based ALgorithm for Quantum OptimizatioN (FALQON) is a hybrid quantum-classical algorithm for solving combinatorial optimization problems, which circumvents classical parameter optimization but requires a deep quantum circuit. To reduce circuit depth, Arai et al. proposed second-order FALQON (SO-FALQON), achieving the best depth reduction among existing approaches. However, SO-FALQON brings a 2.3 times per-step measurement overhead, as it needs to calculate an additional second-order control coefficient. In this paper, inspired by the Backtracking Line Search (BLS) theory, we propose another method called BLS-FALQON, which not only reduces circuit depth to a comparable extent, but also achieves fewer measurements than SO-FALQON. Numerical simulations on max-cut problem with 8 to 20 vertices demonstrate that BLS-FALQON reduces the total measurement count by 37.7% compared to SO-FALQON, while maintaining a comparable circuit depth. Furthermore, we conduct real quantum hardware experiments on the Tianyan-176 quantum computer, which uses the zuchongzhi2 superconducting quantum processor, confirming that BLS-FALQON remains effective under real quantum hardware conditions.

</details>


### [95] [High-fidelity geometric quantum gates exceeding 99.9% in germanium quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33251)
*Yu-Chen Zhou,Rong-Long Ma,Zhenzhen Kong,Ao-Ran Li,Chengxian Zhang,Xin Zhang,Yang Liu,Hao-Tian Jiang,Zhi-Tao Wu,Gui-Lei Wang,Gang Cao,Guang-Can Guo,Hai-Ou Li,Guo-Ping Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在锗量子点中实现单空穴自旋量子比特，通过门集层析发现非共振噪声限制性能，引入几何量子计算实现抗噪高保真操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现高保真且鲁棒的量子比特操控是容错量子计算的关键要求。门集层析分析表明连续I门期间的非共振噪声严重限制了量子比特性能，需寻找抗噪的操控方法。

Method: 在锗量子点中实现单空穴自旋量子比特，使用门集层析（GST）表征控制保真度；针对非共振噪声问题，引入几何量子计算实现抗噪的量子比特操控。

Result: 最大控制保真度在I、X/2、Y/2门分别达到97.48%、99.81%、99.88%。几何门控制保真度在宽Rabi频率范围内保持在99%以上，最高超过99.9%。微波频率失谐±2.5 MHz（±1.2 MHz）时，几何X/2和Y/2（I）门保真度仍超过99%。

Conclusion: 几何量子计算是实现半导体量子计算中可重复高保真量子比特操控的潜在方法。

Abstract: Achieving high-fidelity and robust qubit manipulations is a crucial requirement for realizing faulttolerant quantum computation. Here, we demonstrate a single-hole spin qubit in a germanium quantum dot and characterize its control fidelity using gate set tomography. The maximum control fidelities reach 97.48%, 99.81%, 99.88% for the I, X/2 and Y /2 gate, respectively. These results reveal that off-resonance noise during consecutive I gates in gate set tomography sequences severely limits qubit performance. Therefore, we introduce geometric quantum computation to realize noiseresilient qubit manipulation. The geometric gate control fidelities remain above 99% across a wide range of Rabi frequencies. The maximum fidelity surpasses 99.9%. Furthermore, the fidelities of geometric X/2 and Y /2 (I) gates exceed 99% even when detuning the microwave frequency by +-2.5 MHz (+-1.2 MHz), highlighting the noise-resilient feature. These results demonstrate that geometric quantum computation is a potential method for achieving high-fidelity qubit manipulation reproducibly in semiconductor quantum computation.

</details>


### [96] [An exponential strong converse for private communication over degradable quantum channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32515)
*Mark M. Wilde*

Main category: quant-ph

Abstract: We prove an exponential strong converse for the unassisted private capacity of every finite-dimensional degradable quantum channel. We use a single error criterion: the infidelity between the actual message-estimate-environment state and an ideal state consisting of uniformly distributed, perfectly correlated messages that are independent of the environment. At every rate strictly above the channel's maximum coherent information, the optimized fidelity to this ideal state decays exponentially in the number of channel uses. The same bound holds when the ideal environment state is fixed to the actual environment marginal. The proof applies to arbitrary mixed-state encoders and arbitrary collective decoders. Its main operator ingredient is an overlap bound for privacy tests: two receiver placements with a common subsystem of dimension $d_C$ have overlap at most $d_C/M$, where $M$ is the key size, independently of both shield dimensions. We combine this bound with the signed averaging construction presented in arXiv:2608.01308 and a single-filter reduction to symmetric states. A Gaussian filter replaces the common receiver subsystem by a system of controlled dimension from which the original correlations can be approximately recovered using quantum side information. The filter preserves exact exchange symmetry, and a uniform smooth-entropy estimate bounds its output dimension for arbitrary correlated channel inputs. We also obtain an exponential strong converse at zero for antidegradable channels under the same joint criterion and, consequently, for the private capacity of the quantum erasure channel throughout its parameter range.

</details>

Abstract: Point defects in wide-bandgap semiconductors offer spin and photonic qubits in a solid-state platform, making them important building blocks for quantum information processing, communication, and sensing. While these systems have the strong advantage of room-temperature operation, intrinsic electron-phonon interaction induces broad phonon-sideband emission and weak zero-phonon-line transitions, limiting efficient spin-photon interfaces and scalable photon-mediated interaction. Here, we introduce a controlled heterophase interface based on the remote epitaxy technique as a crystal heterogeneity-engineering strategy. While stacking faults are treated as imperfections to be eliminated, our results instead show that crystal-phase interfaces can provide an additional degree of freedom to engineer defect-phonon interaction beyond the intrinsic properties of a single crystal. In comparison with single-phase 4H-silicon carbide (SiC) membranes, Vsi in 3C/4H heterointerface SiC exhibits a drastic enhancement of zero-phonon optical transitions, with a substantially narrower zero-phonon linewidth of 4.71 meV even at room temperature. These results establish heterophase-interface engineering as an effective route for tailoring defect-phonon interactions and realizing bright room-temperature quantum emitters with enhanced zero-phonon optical transitions.

</details>

Conclusion: 结果为 monolithic 固态量子储能与能量传输提供了有用见解，明确了最优充电机制及几何/耦合条件。

Abstract: The waveguide-integrated architecture holds promise for scalable on-chip integration of a charger, a transmission channel, and a quantum battery in a modular, independently optimizable design. We investigate a minimal charging configuration consisting of two emitters coupled to a semi-infinite waveguide and identify the optimal charging mechanism, wherein non-Markovian dynamics driven by out-of-band bound states give rise to single-frequency oscillations of the stored energy. We show that near-unity charging efficiency is achievable at weak to moderate coupling strengths via detuning control, provided that the battery-to-boundary separation $n$ is less than three times the charger-to-boundary separation $m$ ($n<3m$)---a constraint imposed by the mirror-induced feedback---while the average charging power decays exponentially with the spatial separation $n-m$. These findings offer useful insights for monolithic solid-state quantum energy storage and transport.

</details>


### [99] [Beyond NISQ Assumptions: One-time Memory in the Classically Accessible Random-Oracle Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32603)
*Boyang Chen,Tianren Liu,Luojian Wei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在经典可访问随机预言机模型（CAROM）下，仅用BB84态构造了高效的模拟安全一次性存储器（OTM），其模拟优势随参数n指数衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息可实现经典世界不可能的密码原语。已有工作基于NISQ量子对手受限的假设构建了强一次性功能协议，但早期容错量子计算机将允许更深的逻辑量子电路，动摇了这些NISQ假设的适用性。因此需要寻找在NISQ乃至早期容错时代仍合理的假设。

Method: 采用经典可访问随机预言机模型（CAROM，源自[BDF+11]和[AK22]），限制对手只能经典查询随机预言机——该限制对NISQ量子对手动机充分，在早期容错量子计算机场景下也可能成立。基于CAROM构造模拟安全的一次性存储器（OTM）协议，仅使用BB84态，通信量为二次型：对λ比特消息和参数n=n(λ)、ℓ=ℓ(λ)，使用nℓ个量子比特和(n+2)λ个经典比特。

Result: 对于经典查询随机预言机至多2^{ℓ/2-1}-1次的任意量子对手，其模拟优势至多为(n+3)(3/4)^n，即随n指数级小。

Conclusion: 在CAROM下可实现高效的模拟安全一次性存储器，仅需BB84态，且模拟优势随参数n指数衰减，适用于NISQ及早期容错量子计算时代。

Abstract: Quantum information enables many cryptographic primitives that are impossible in the classical world. A line of works has developed cryptographic protocol under the assumption that quantum adversaries are restricted to noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) computing power, enabling strong one-time functionalities. But the advent of early fault-tolerant quantum computers eras will allow deeper logical quantum circuits, calling into questions the applicability of these NISQ-based assumptions.
  In this work, we adapt the classically accessible random oracle model (CAROM) as in [BDF+11] and [AK22], in which adversaries are only allowed to classically query the random oracle. The restriction is well motivated for NISQ quantum adversaries and may remain plausible in the presence of early fault-tolerant quantum computers. Then, we show that an efficient simulation-secure one-time memory (OTM) is possible under CAROM. Our protocol uses only BB84 states and has quadratic communication: for a $λ$-bit message and integer-valued parameters $n=n(λ)$ and $\ell=\ell(λ)$, the construction uses $n\ell$ qubits and $(n+2)λ$ classical bits, and for any quantum adversary with at most $2^{\ell/2-1}-1$ classical queries to the random oracle, its simulation advantage is at most $(n+3)\left(\frac{3}{4}\right)^n.$ Hence exponentially small simulation advantage in $n$.

</details>


### [100] [Trotterisation Thresholds and Dissipative Quantum Chaos in Open-System Digital Quantum Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34623)
*Angsar Manatuly,Lukas M. Sieberer,Cahit Kargi,Nathan K. Langford*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Trotter化数字量子模拟（DQS）的阈值研究从封闭系统拓展到开放系统，证明即使存在耗散与退相干，Trotter化阈值依然存在，并基于随机矩阵理论（RMT）提出统计方法以刻画阈值后的量子混沌onset，揭示耗散强度需与底层幺正混沌相平衡时才会出现耗散型量子混沌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Trotter化DQS中增大Trotter步长可减少门数，但超过某阈值会因量子混沌导致精度骤降；此前该阈值行为仅在无环境噪声与退相干的封闭系统中被研究，而真实量子计算机对此高度敏感，故需研究开放系统中的阈值行为。

Method: 研究一系列实验相关的开放系统量子模拟模型；引入基于随机矩阵理论（RMT）的统计技术，借鉴封闭系统方法，严格刻画Trotter化阈值之后的近似幺正混沌与真正耗散型量子混沌的onset。

Result: 开放系统Trotter化阈值依然存在，包括在长时、稳态极限（所有相干性已丢失）下；开放DQS在阈值前能准确复现开放目标系统动力学，但超过阈值后仍出现性能骤降；耗散型量子混沌在耗散与底层幺正量子混沌相平衡时出现——耗散需强到足以诱导真正耗散行为，又不能强到抑制底层混沌结构。

Conclusion: Trotter化阈值是开放系统DQS的普遍现象，且可用RMT统计方法严格表征；有效的开放DQS要求耗散强度处于既能产生耗散混沌又不压制幺正混沌的平衡区间。

Abstract: Digital quantum simulation (DQS) is one of the most promising applications of quantum computing. Trotterised DQS models quantum dynamics by decomposing the evolution into a sequence of gate-based Trotter steps. Using larger Trotter steps reduces the required gate count, but it is now understood that the Trotterisation step size cannot be increased beyond a certain threshold value, beyond which there is a sudden breakdown in simulation accuracy, due to the onset of quantum chaos. To date, this behaviour has only been studied for closed systems not subjected to the environmental noise and decoherence to which real-world quantum computers are highly sensitive. In this work, we study a range of experimentally relevant open-system quantum simulator models and show that Trotterisation thresholds persist even when open-system effects are included, even in the long-time, steady-state limit, once all coherence has been lost. We show that open DQS can accurately reproduce the dynamics of open target systems prior to the threshold, but still exhibits a sudden performance breakdown beyond it. We introduce new, statistical techniques to rigorously characterise the onset of quantum chaos beyond the Trotterisation threshold for both approximately unitary and genuine dissipative quantum chaos, based on random matrix theory (RMT), building on techniques developed for closed systems. Finally, we demonstrate that dissipative quantum chaos emerges when the dissipation is in balance with the presence of underlying unitary quantum chaos; the dissipation must be strong enough to induce genuinely dissipative behaviour, yet not so strong as to suppress the underlying chaotic structure.

</details>


### [101] [Fewer Qubits, Better Choices: Coupling-Aware Sub-QUBO Selection for Quantum-Assisted Traffic Zone Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32627)
*Qianwen Guo,Ruimin Ke*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文指出近端量子优化器分解策略中“按单变量翻转收益排序”的选变量规则在局部最优时失效，提出基于二阶展开的选变量方法，将其建模为奖赏收集型最密子图问题并给出贪心线性时间算法与两个无求解器界，实验表明收益主要来自选变量而非后端量子硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近端量子优化器仅能容纳约百个二值变量，大规模QUBO问题须分解为硬件可处理的子问题，其余变量保持不变。现有方法通常按单个变量翻转引起的目标变化排序来选变量，但当当前解在单变量翻转下已最优时，该排序失去信息量：所有得分都只反映代价，而真正的改进依赖被忽略的成对交互项。因此需要更有效的子问题变量选择准则。

Method: 对目标函数做二阶展开来刻画被忽略的成对交互，将选变量问题转化为奖赏收集型最密子图（prize-collecting densest-subgraph）选择问题；采用贪心算法求解，时间复杂度与变量数成线性；并给出两个无需调用求解器的界，用于夹逼所选子集内可达的改进量。在芝加哥与费城的交通网络上进行实验，对比所提规则与随机选择的子问题规模效应，并以几何邻接与路网连通性作为两种选变量依据，同时固定选择轨迹、改变在120量子比特设备上求解子问题的比例。

Result: 在芝加哥和费城交通网络上，使用16变量子问题的所提规则优于使用64变量子问题的随机选择，说明四倍设备容量无法弥补较弱的选变量策略；将几何邻接替换为路网连通性后优势扩大1.6–1.9倍。固定选择轨迹，将在120量子比特设备上求解子问题的比例从0变到1，最终目标值在六位有效数字内保持不变。硬件可行性受耦合项限制：120变量问题在7,260项时无法编译，5,118项时可编译；编译时间随项数近似二次增长并主导执行成本，达1,752秒，而量子处理时间为469秒。

Conclusion: 在硬件可及规模下，量子优化分解的收益主要来自子问题变量选择而非量子后端本身。基于二阶交互的选变量规则明显优于单变量翻转排序和随机选择，且路网连通性优于几何邻接。硬件可行性受耦合项数量制约，编译成本随项数近似二次增长并成为主要开销，因此提升选变量质量比单纯增加设备容量更关键。

Abstract: Near-term quantum optimizers accommodate roughly a hundred binary variables, requiring large Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization problems to be decomposed into hardware-sized subproblems while other variables remain fixed. Variables are typically selected by ranking the objective changes from individual flips. We show that this rule is uninformative once the incumbent is optimal under single-variable flips: every score represents a cost, while remaining improvements depend on overlooked pairwise interactions. A second-order expansion instead yields a prize-collecting densest-subgraph selection problem, solvable greedily in time linear in the number of variables, and two solver-free bounds bracketing the improvement achievable within a selected subset. On transportation networks from Chicago and Philadelphia, our rule with 16-variable subproblems outperforms random selection with 64, demonstrating that quadrupling device capacity cannot compensate for weaker selection. Replacing geometric adjacency with road-network connectivity widens the margin by a factor of 1.6-1.9. With the selection trace fixed, varying the fraction of subproblems solved on a 120-qubit device from zero to one leaves the final objective unchanged to six significant figures. At tested hardware-accessible sizes, gains therefore arise from selection rather than the backend. Hardware feasibility is limited by coupling terms: 120-variable problems fail to compile with 7,260 terms but succeed with 5,118. Compilation scales almost quadratically with term count and dominates execution cost, reaching 1,752 seconds versus 469 seconds of quantum processing.

</details>


### [102] [Improving the Stability of the Hierarchical Equations of Motion for Open Quantum Systems with Strong Coupling to Structured Bosonic Baths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35484)
*Salvatore Gatto,Samuel L. Rudge,Bokang Hou,Eran Rabani,Michael Thoss*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对HEOM方法在玻色环境中因有限截断导致的数值不稳定问题，利用辅助Fock空间图像识别导致瞬态放大的非平衡升阶项，并通过非幺正相似变换将其转化为升降阶平衡组合，从而显著拓展可模拟的系统-环境耦合强度范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HEOM是模拟开放量子系统动力学的强有力方法，在许多参数区间和物理场景下稳定高效，但玻色环境中层次方程的有限截断会不可避免地带根本性数值不稳定问题，限制了可模拟的参数范围。

Method: 采用辅助Fock空间图像描述层次结构，识别出导致强瞬态放大的不平衡升阶项（其根源是HEOM生成元的非正规代数结构）；随后应用非幺正相似变换，将这些不平衡项转化为升阶与降阶项的平衡组合，以抑制其数值影响。先在具有简单Brownian振子谱密度的自旋-玻色模型中验证，再推广至结构化谱密度。

Result: 在简单Brownian振子谱密度和结构化谱密度两种情形下，变换后的HEOM方法均能实现比原始方法大得多的系统-环境耦合强度模拟，有效抑制了层次截断引起的数值不稳定。

Conclusion: 通过辅助Fock空间分析与非幺正相似变换平衡升降阶项，可显著提升HEOM在玻色环境中的数值稳定性，拓展其可处理的耦合强度范围，为开放量子系统动力学模拟提供更鲁棒的工具。

Abstract: The hierarchical equations of motion (HEOM) approach is one of the most powerful methods avail- able to simulate the dynamics of open quantum systems. Although the HEOM approach has been shown to be stable and efficient in many parameter regimes and physical scenarios, it is also well known that the unavoidable finite truncation of the hierarchy can lead to fundamental numeri- cal instabilities for bosonic environments. In this work, we apply recently developed techniques to analyze these issues. We use an auxiliary Fock-space picture of the hierarchy to first identify the unbalanced hierarchy-raising terms responsible for the strong transient amplification associated with the non-normal algebraic structure of the HEOM generator. We then apply a non-unitary similarity transformation that converts these terms into balanced combinations of hierarchy-raising and hierarchy-lowering terms, thereby suppressing their numerical impact. We first demonstrate the efficacy of this approach via a spin-boson model with a simple Brownian oscillator spectral den- sity and subsequently extend the analysis to a structured spectral density, showing for both cases that the transformed HEOM approach allows simulations of much larger system-bath coupling than would otherwise be possible.

</details>


### [103] [Exact Gaussian Entanglement Dynamics and Initial-State Control in Coupled Parametric Oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32709)
*Roberto Bernal-Jaquez,Hector H. Hernandez-Hernandez,Alexander Schaum,Guillermo Chacon-Acosta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究由公共时变弹簧驱动的双线性耦合谐振子：通过正则模变换化为两个参量振子，用Lewis-Riesenfeld不变量与Ermakov-Pinney振幅精确求解，给出闭合形式的两模协方差矩阵与对数负度，并澄清Duan-Simon判据的性质及纠缠调控机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确描述含时驱动耦合高斯系统的纠缠生成，并厘清纠缠判据（Duan-Simon）与纠缠度量之间的关系，同时区分真正的可分离制备态与耦合哈密顿量的本征纠缠基态，为实验可控的纠缠调控提供理论依据。

Method: 采用固定正则模变换把双线性耦合谐振子对角化为两个参量振子，分别用Lewis-Riesenfeld不变量和Ermakov-Pinney振幅精确求解（含升降算符构造与Lewis-Riesenfeld相位）。对不变量真空输入求闭合形式的物理两模协方差矩阵；用对数负度刻画纠缠，并对Duan-Simon比较进行显式归一化分析（含局域辛优化）；最后用SU(1,1)表述把2E_N与SU(1,1)/U(1)圆盘上两正则模压缩轨迹的双曲距离对应起来，并作数值模拟。

Result: 对数负度坍缩为 E_N = (1/2) arcosh(X/2)，其中单一无量纲不变量 X ≥ 2 度量两正则模的相对相空间形变。经局域辛优化后，Duan检验与本族纯交换对称高斯态的PPT具有相同纠缠阈值，但优化Duan量为见证而非独立度量，满足 D_opt/D_sep = e^{-E_N}。仅用真正可分离的乘积制备态主张纠缠生成。正弦刚度调制下，可分离输入在共振外呈有界关联、在参量共振附近持续增长；数值表明初始宽度与啁啾可作为两个独立且实验可及的目标时刻纠缠控制量。SU(1,1)表述显示纠缠同时由相对压缩幅度与相对压缩角决定，并给出幅度近乎相等却因角度产生强纠缠的区制。

Conclusion: 该工作为含时驱动耦合高斯系统提供了精确、闭合形式的纠缠刻画，明确了Duan-Simon判据在此族中仅作见证而阈值与PPT一致，并揭示纠缠可由压缩幅度与压缩角共同调控，特别是角度单独即可产生强纠缠，为实验上通过初始宽度与啁啾控制纠缠提供了理论支持。

Abstract: We study two bilinearly coupled harmonic oscillators driven by a common time-dependent spring. A fixed normal-mode transformation reduces the pair to two parametric oscillators, each solved exactly by its Lewis-Riesenfeld invariant and Ermakov-Pinney amplitude, including the ladder-operator construction and the Lewis-Riesenfeld phase. For invariant-vacuum inputs the physical two-mode covariance matrix is obtained in closed form, and the logarithmic negativity collapses to $E_{N}=\frac{1}{2} \operatorname{arcosh}(X/2)$, where a single dimensionless invariant $X \geq 2$ measures the relative phase-space deformation of the two normal modes. We give a normalization-explicit account of the Duan--Simon comparison: after local symplectic optimization the Duan test and the positive partial transpose share the entanglement threshold for this pure exchange-symmetric Gaussian family, but the optimized Duan quantity is a witness, not an independent measure, with $D_{\rm opt}/D_{\rm sep}=e^{-E_N}$. We carefully separate a genuinely separable product preparation from the already-entangled ground state of the coupled Hamiltonian, and use only the former for claims of entanglement generation. Under sinusoidal stiffness modulation a separable input shows bounded correlations away from resonance and sustained growth near parametric resonance, and we demonstrate numerically that the initial width and chirp act as two independent, experimentally accessible controls of the entanglement at a chosen target time. An $SU(1,1)$ formulation identifies $2 E_{N}$ with the hyperbolic separation of the two normal-mode squeezing trajectories on the $SU(1,1)/U(1)$ disk and shows that entanglement is governed by both the relative squeezing magnitude and the relative squeezing angle; ; we exhibit regimes in which nearly equal magnitudes still produce strong entanglement through the angle alone.

</details>


### [104] [Electron decoherence in cylindrical holes and circular apertures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35498)
*Cruz I. Velasco,F. Javier García de Abajo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了快电子在两种典型几何结构（金属圆柱孔与理想导体薄膜圆孔）中的退相干定量理论，结合电磁格林张量与涨落-耗散定理并保留推迟效应，给出退相干概率与弹性相位随轨迹、温度和材料响应的解析表达，并指出退相干对相干电子束仪器设计的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由电子的相干性决定了相位敏感电子显微镜的分辨率与衬度，而电子一旦在环境中留下可区分的激发就会丧失相干性。因此需要定量理解快电子在不同几何环境中的退相干机制及其对探测分辨率的限制。

Method: 采用电磁格林张量表述并结合涨落-耗散定理，完整保留推迟效应，计算退相干概率和弹性相位；对圆柱孔给出闭式、按方位角分辨的解析解；对圆孔使用柱面波模式展开求解；分析其高频/高温及高电导极限行为。

Result: 圆柱孔情形得到分离路径位置、温度与电导依赖的闭式方位分辨表达式，并在高电导、高温极限下化为与电导和电子速度无关、线性于温度的普适形式。圆孔情形下能量损失概率在低频按1/ω发散，而退相干概率保持有限，并在孔径与相关电磁波长可比时达到最大。由于相互作用空间局域化，圆孔引起的退相干一般弱于圆柱孔，除非在高温下孔径足够大。

Conclusion: 对足够大的孔，退相干可主导聚焦电子探针的空间展宽，因此必须在相干电子束仪器设计中加以考虑。

Abstract: The coherence of free electrons sets fundamental limits on the resolution and contrast of phase-sensitive electron microscopy because coherence is lost whenever the electron leaves a distinguishing excitation in its environment. We develop a quantitative theory of fast-electron decoherence in two canonical geometries: a cylindrical hole drilled through a realistic metal and a circular aperture in a thin perfectly conducting film. By combining an electromagnetic Green-tensor formulation with the fluctuation--dissipation theorem while fully retaining retardation, we obtain the decoherence probability and elastic phase as functions of the electron trajectory, temperature, and material response. For the cylindrical hole, we obtain a closed-form, azimuthally resolved expression that separates the dependences on path position, temperature, and conductivity. In the high-conductivity and high-temperature limits, this result reduces to a universal expression that is linear in temperature and independent of both conductivity and electron velocity. For the circular aperture, which we solve using a cylindrical-wave modal expansion, the energy-loss probability diverges as $1/ω$ at low frequency, whereas the decoherence probability remains finite and reaches a maximum when the aperture radius is comparable to the relevant electromagnetic wavelength. Because the interaction is spatially localized, aperture-induced decoherence is generally weaker than that produced by a cylindrical hole, except for sufficiently large apertures at high temperature. Finally, we show that, for sufficiently large holes, decoherence can dominate the spatial broadening of a focused electron probe and must therefore be incorporated into the design of coherent electron-beam instruments.

</details>


### [105] [Generation of arbitrary quantum states in cavity-atom systems with a three-level ancilla](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32845)
*Da-Wei Luo,Ting Yu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推广Law与Eberly的经典工作，提出一种以三能级原子为辅助（ancilla）来生成任意量子态的新协议，通过在每个控制步骤中并行移动两个激发，将演化步骤数减半，并给出控制场强度与时间安排的完整解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Law与Eberly的原始方案在生成任意量子态时需要的演化步骤较多、所需时间较长，导致控制场时间与幅度的误差有更多累积机会，限制了方案效率与鲁棒性，因此需要一种更高效的协议。

Method: 将Law-Eberly方案进行推广，采用三能级原子作为辅助系统；设计控制协议使每个控制步骤中同时并行转移两个激发，从而把总体演化步骤数减少一半；对控制场强度与作用时刻推导完整的解析表达式；并进行量子速度极限（quantum speed limit）背景下的定量分析。

Result: 演化步骤数较原方案减半；获得了控制场强度与定时的完整解析解；系统动力学所面临的时序与幅度误差累积概率降低；并结合量子速度极限给出了定量研究结果。

Conclusion: 该协议在保持生成任意量子态能力的同时显著提升了效率与抗误差累积能力，其解析表达式与量子速度极限分析为基于三能级辅助原子的量子态制备提供了更优且更可量化的实现途径。

Abstract: We generalized the seminal work of Law & Eberly, and propose a protocol to generate arbitrary quantum states with a three-level atom as an ancilla in a more efficient manner. This protocol moves two excitations in parallel in each control step, thus reducing the number of evolution steps by half. Full analytical expressions for the control field strengths and timings are derived. The system dynamics is exposed to less chance of error accumulation in the timing and amplitudes of the control fields, and quantitative study in the context of the quantum speed limit is also considered.

</details>


### [106] [Quantum Machine Learning for Cybersecurity Applications: Simulation and Hardware Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32911)
*Zirui Zhu,Zisheng Chen,Xiangyang Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合架构：用紧凑MLP压缩特征后送入少量量子比特的量子头（QSVM与VQC），在NSL-KDD与Ling-Spam两个安全任务上与同等参数预算的经典模型对比，并在IBM量子设备上做噪声感知验证。结果表明浅层量子头可匹配经典模型，并在近边界难例上略降漏报与误报；真实硬件行为贴近仿真，差距主要来自器件噪声；对抗攻击测试进一步评估了QSVM鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在特征与算力预算紧张时，经典威胁检测流水线在接近决策边界的样本上性能下降。小型量子处理器已可用，但既有工作未充分说明在资源受限条件下量子组件能否提升端到端威胁检测性能，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 提出混合架构：以紧凑多层感知机（MLP）层压缩数据信息，再将处理后特征路由至少数量子比特的量子头，分别采用量子支持向量机（QSVM）和变分量子电路（VQC）实现。在仿真平台上，与参数预算可比的经典模型在两个代表性网络安全任务（NSL-KDD网络入侵检测、Ling-Spam垃圾邮件过滤）上基准对比。并将最优4量子比特QSVM模型通过噪声感知执行部署到IBM Quantum设备，在较小子数据集上验证。此外对其中一个QSVM模型开展对抗攻击测试。

Result: 浅层量子头持续达到与经典模型相当的水平，在困难的近边界样本上略微减少漏报攻击与误报。硬件验证结果与仿真行为贴合较好，剩余差距主要由器件噪声而非模型设计造成。

Conclusion: 即使在规模小且含噪声的设备上，经过精心设计的量子组件也可作为实用网络威胁检测中具有竞争力、预算感知的组成部分。后续结论，可包括混合量子-经典架构在资源受限安全场景中的可行性，以及噪声缓解对硬件落地的重要性。

Abstract: Under tight feature and compute budgets, classical threat detection pipelines often degrade on near-decision-boundary events. Small quantum processors are now available, but existing work inadequately shows whether quantum components improve end-to-end threat detection under the above resource constrained conditions. This paper tries to address this gap with a hybrid architecture that uses a compact multilayer perceptron layer to compress the information in data and then routes the processed features to a few qubit quantum heads implemented in quantum support vector machine (QSVM) and variational quantum circuit (VQC) models. On a simulation platform, we benchmark these hybrid models against classical models with comparable parameter budgets on two representative cybersecurity tasks, network intrusion detection on NSL-KDD dataset and spam filtering on Ling-Spam dataset. To validate their precision on real quantum hardware, we deploy the best 4-qubit QSVM model on an IBM Quantum device with noise-aware execution, evaluated on a smaller sub-dataset. In the results, shallow quantum heads consistently match, and on difficult near-boundary cases modestly reduce missed attacks and false alarms compared to classical models using the same features. Hardware validation results track the simulation behavior closely enough that the remaining gap is dominated by device noise rather than model design. Furthermore, we conduct adversarial attacks to test the robustness of one QSVM model. Taken together, the study shows that even on small, noisy devices, carefully engineered quantum components may function as competitive, budget-aware components in practical cyber threat detection applications.

</details>


### [107] [The wiring sets a phase-blind quantum memory's gap, the weight caps its coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32943)
*Ruini Qian,Zhaobin Lyu,Zelong Yin,Jingjing Hu,Dengfeng Li,Shuoming An*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究表明，在跳跃重置单一基态的耗散机制中，连通方式（wiring）可调节记忆的gap time而不改变相位衰减速率；dephasing下gap time跨两个数量级，相位不随之变化。此类系统中，涉及两个及以上量子比特的contrast比裸量子比特衰减更快，且功率泵入无法替代，其上限为2除以contrast跨越的比特数；双支泵在距离3时可突破该上限，双时钟测试可在已发表数据上运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程化耗散虽能纠正错误，但会引入记忆相位衰减，而"记忆的gap"究竟度量什么尚无定论。作者旨在厘清gap time与相位衰减速率之间的关系，并检验其受连通方式与泵浦功率的影响。

Method: 设定每次跳跃重置一个基态，比较不同连通方式（wiring）下gap time与相位衰减速率的独立性；在dephasing条件下考察gap time随连通方式的变化；分析多量子比特contrast的衰减上限；并采用双支泵（two-branch pump）与双时钟测试（two-clock test）进行检验。

Result: 连通方式可设定gap time，而相位保持其速率不变；dephasing下gap time跨两个数量级，相位不变。在此类系统中，跨两个或多个量子比特的contrast比裸量子比特衰减更快，且无泵浦功率可弥补；上限为2除以contrast跨越的量子比特数。双支泵在距离3时可突破该上限，双时钟测试可在已发表数据上运行。

Conclusion: 记忆的gap time与相位衰减速率在该类耗散机制中相互独立，gap time可由连通方式调节，而contrast的衰减受比特数决定的上限约束；双支泵提供突破该上限的途径，并通过双时钟测试在既数据中得到验证。

Abstract: Engineered dissipation corrects errors, yet a memory's phase decays. What the memory's gap measures is not settled. Here we show that when each jump resets one basis state, the wiring can set the gap time while the phase keeps its rate. Under dephasing the gap time spans two orders across wirings; the phase does not. In this class, a contrast on two or more qubits fades faster than a bare qubit at any distance; no such pump power substitutes. The ceiling is two over the qubits the contrast spans. A two-branch pump can escape it at distance three; a two-clock test runs on published data.

</details>


### [108] [Toroidal-Core Certificates for PPT-Squared Diagonal Orthogonal Covariant Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32979)
*Samuel Marquez Gonzalez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对DOC类量子信道的PPT-squared猜想，给出了一个充分条件：当两个信道对应的关联矩阵满足逐元素混合不等式时，其复合必为纠缠破坏（EB）信道；在四维情形给出了通用系数4，并构造了一族两两复合均为EB但每个成员本身非EB的双随机PPT信道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPT-squared猜想（两个PPT量子信道的复合是否必为EB）在一般情况下仍未解决，在DOC（对角正交协变）类中，第一个未解决的确定性维度是四。作者希望在此类中寻找可判定的充分条件，并厘清相干性数据在复合后何时导致纠缠破坏。

Method: 针对任意有限维DOC映射推导PPT-squared复合的充分条件。证明将量子相干数据分离为两个关联矩阵，分别与单位矩阵混合直至落入秩一关联矩阵的凸包，再将所得对象转化为显式的三重完全正（TCP）核心；剩余部分为纯经典贡献，当满足简单的逐元素混合不等式时即为TCP。对M_d上的PPT DOC映射Φ_{A,B,C}与Φ_{D,E,F}，给出逐元素界(AD)_{ij}≥2⌊√d⌋√(A_{ii}A_{jj}D_{ii}D_{jj})以保证复合为EB；四维时通用系数为4；并作秩敏感的精细化，说明低相干秩如何改善通用系数。论文还构造了一族连续的双随机PPT信道。

Result: 在满足上述逐元素不等式时，Φ_{A,B,C}∘Φ_{D,E,F}必为EB；四维情形通用系数为4；构造的连续双随机PPT信道族中，每个a≠1的成员均非EB，但族内任意两两复合均被证明为EB；秩敏感精细化显式刻画了低相干秩对通用系数的改进。

Conclusion: 所得证书是充分而非必要的，与因子宽度方法和半定层级方法互补。该结果在DOC类中给出了可操作的PPT-squared判据，并揭示了相干秩与混合不等式在决定复合纠缠破坏性中的作用。

Abstract: The positive-partial-transpose-squared (PPT-squared) conjecture asks whether the composition of two positive-partial-transpose (PPT) quantum channels must be entanglement breaking (EB). The problem remains open in general and, within the diagonal orthogonal covariant (DOC) class, the first unresolved deterministic dimension is four. A sufficient condition for PPT-squared composition is derived for DOC maps in arbitrary finite dimension. The proof isolates the quantum coherence data into two correlation matrices, mixes each with the identity until it lies in the convex hull of rank-one correlation matrices, and converts the resulting objects into an explicit triplewise completely positive (TCP) core. The remaining contribution is purely classical and is TCP whenever a simple entrywise mixing inequality is satisfied. For PPT DOC maps $Φ_{A,B,C}$ and $Φ_{D,E,F}$ on $M_d$, the entrywise bound $(AD)_{ij}\ge 2\lfloor\sqrt{d}\rfloor\sqrt{A_{ii}A_{jj}D_{ii}D_{jj}}$ for all $i,j$ guarantees that $Φ_{A,B,C}\circΦ_{D,E,F}$ is EB. In dimension four the universal coefficient becomes four. A continuous family of bistochastic PPT channels is then constructed; every member with $a\ne1$ is not EB, while every pairwise composition within the family is proved to be EB. A rank-sensitive refinement shows explicitly how low coherence rank can improve the universal coefficient. The resulting certificate is sufficient rather than necessary and is complementary to factor-width and semidefinite-hierarchy approaches.

</details>


### [109] [Loss-Resilient Quantum Networking with Dicke Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33094)
*Bowen Wang,Hongyu Yang,Jiacheng Sun,Wanping Zhou,Jizhou Wu,Yunlong Xiao,Dian Wu,Jian Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次制备八光子半激发Dicke态（保真度0.922(11)），并据此实现开放目的地隐形传态与量子远程克隆两种网络任务，揭示二者可统一于同一操作框架，且协议在损耗与信息缺失下仍超越经典极限与GHZ基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体纠缠可为量子网络赋予经典不可实现的能力，但其在物理与信息获取逐步受限条件下的演化规律尚缺乏实验探索；同时需要一种在损耗、探测不完美与部分代理缺席时仍保持功能的网络纠缠架构。

Method: 实验制备首个八光子半激发Dicke态，测得保真度0.922(11)；以该态为资源实施两类网络任务——开放目的地隐形传态（任一代理向七个远端代理之一传送未知态）与量子远程克隆（分布式产生七个近似克隆）；并系统考察不完美光子探测、传输损耗及参与代理信息缺失条件下的性能，与GHZ基准方案进行对比。

Result: 隐形传态保真度达0.926(7)-0.941(6)；七个克隆的保真度为0.713(3)，接近理论最优值5/7；协议在探测不完美、传输损耗与代理信息缺失下仍高于经典极限，并优于对应的GHZ基准；随着参与代理数减少，隐形传态保真度呈现阶梯式层级下降，其两端分别退化为开放目的地隐形传态与最优远程克隆。

Conclusion: Dicke纠缠可作为抗损耗量子网络的有前景架构，将开放目的地隐形传态与量子远程克隆纳入统一操作框架，为在现实运行条件下仍保持有用功能的量子技术开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Multipartite entanglement can endow a quantum network with capabilities unavailable classically, yet their evolution under progressively reduced physical and informational access remains largely unexplored. Here we realize the first eight-photon half-excitation Dicke state, with a fidelity of 0.922(11), and use it to implement two distinct network tasks: open-destination teleportation and quantum telecloning. An unknown state can be teleported from one agent to any of seven remote agents with fidelities of 0.926(7)-0.941(6), while seven approximate clones are distributed with a fidelity of 0.713(3), close to the optimal value of 5/7. The protocol remains above the classical limit under imperfect photon detection, transmission loss and missing information from participating agents, and outperforms the corresponding GHZ benchmarks. As fewer agents contribute, the teleportation fidelity falls through a stepwise hierarchy whose two extremes recover open-destination teleportation and optimal telecloning, connecting both tasks within a single operational framework. These results establish Dicke entanglement as a promising architecture for loss-resilient quantum networks, opening a route towards quantum technologies that retain useful functionality under realistic operating conditions.

</details>


### [110] [The Breakdown of Classical Minicrypt Equivalences in the Quantum-Computation Classical-Communication Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33098)
*Boyang Chen,Yiming Wang,Ziyi Xie*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子计算-经典通信（QCCC）模型下Minicrypt密码世界的结构，通过oracle分离证明经典Minicrypt图景在该模型下崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典密码学中单向函数、伪随机数生成器、承诺、签名等基本原语刻画了同一个"Minicrypt"世界。作者希望探究这一图景在QCCC模型（各方可量子本地计算但仅通过经典消息通信）中能保留到什么程度。

Method: 采用oracle分离方法，构造两个存在高效可验证单向谜题（EV-OWPuzz）的oracle世界（由已知等价关系，二者中QCCC一次性签名也存在）：第一个世界中单向函数不存在（即使允许量子伪确定性求值）；第二个世界中QCCC比特承诺不存在。证明发展了分析随机相位态的技术，包括集中定理与LOCC解耦定理。

Result: 成功构造两个oracle世界证明经典Minicrypt图景在QCCC模型中崩溃：一个世界中单向函数不存在，另一个世界中QCCC比特承诺不存在，而两世界中EV-OWPuzz与QCCC一次性签名均存在。

Conclusion: 经典Minicrypt中从签名到单向函数及比特承诺的归约在QCCC设定下不存在完全黑盒对应物，表明经典Minicrypt结构在QCCC模型中被打破。

Abstract: Classically, it is well-known that several fundamental cryptographic primitives, including one-way functions, pseudorandom number generators, commitments, and signatures, characterize the same cryptographic world, which is known as "Minicrypt". In this paper, we investigate to what extent this picture persists in the quantum-computation classical-communication (QCCC) setting, where parties may perform quantum local computation, but communicate through only classical messages. We demonstrate that the classical Minicrypt landscape breaks down in the QCCC model by oracle separations. We construct two oracle worlds where efficiently verifiable one-way puzzles (EV-OWPuzz) exist. By a known equivalence, QCCC one-time signatures also exist in both worlds. In the first, one-way functions do not exist, even if we allow quantum pseudodeterministic evaluation. In the second, QCCC bit commitments do not exist. Thus, the classical Minicrypt reductions from signatures to one-way functions and bit commitments have no fully black-box counterparts in the QCCC setting. Our proofs develop techniques for analyzing the random phase states, including a concentration theorem and an LOCC decoupling theorem, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [111] [Quantum Monte Carlo Tree Search with Fixed Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33132)
*Mingjie Hu,Jian-Qiang Hu,Enlu Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子计算如何改进固定置信度下的蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS），提出量子MCTS算法（QMCTS），建立了查询复杂度的实例相关下界与对应上界，并通过仿真及真实量子硬件验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算的最新进展为计算密集型决策问题带来了新机遇。本文旨在探究量子计算能否在固定置信度设定下提升MCTS的性能——即以高概率识别博弈树中近似最优动作的同时最小化查询复杂度。

Method: 首先在量子预言机（quantum oracle）模型下对MCTS进行形式化建模；随后提出QMCTS算法，将搜索树上的阈值式淘汰（threshold-based elimination）与量子蒙特卡洛估计相结合，以降低评估随机叶节点值的代价。

Result: 建立了查询复杂度的实例相关（instance-dependent）下界，并推导出QMCTS相应的上界；下界分析中提出了一个新的量子相位测试（quantum phase-testing）结果，可能具有超出MCTS的普适价值；通过仿真实验验证理论结果，并在真实量子硬件上验证了QMCTS的可行性。

Conclusion: QMCTS在理论上达到了与实例相关下界相匹配的查询复杂度上界，仿真与真实量子硬件实验均验证了其有效性与可行性，所提出的量子相位测试结果亦具有更广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Recent advances in quantum computing are opening new opportunities for computationally intensive decision problems. This paper studies how quantum computing can improve Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS) in the fixed-confidence setting, where the goal is to identify a near-optimal move in a given game tree with high probability while minimizing query complexity. We first formulate MCTS under a quantum oracle model. We then develop a quantum MCTS algorithm (QMCTS) that combines threshold-based elimination on the search tree with quantum Monte Carlo estimation to reduce the cost of evaluating stochastic leaf values. We establish an instance-dependent lower bound on the query complexity and derive a corresponding upper bound for QMCTS. The lower-bound analysis introduces a new quantum phase-testing result that may also be useful beyond MCTS. We validate the theoretical results through simulation experiments and further demonstrate the feasibility of QMCTS on real quantum hardware.

</details>


### [112] [Nash Equilibrium from Quantum-Field Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33188)
*Hao Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将纠缠采集与量子博弈耦合：两个Unruh-DeWitt探测器从(3+1)维Minkowski真空标量场采集纠缠，裁判用独立问题比特与相位校准的局域Bell测量将量子态转为支付，证明稳定策略结构随场关联区域变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究关注纠缠采集可把量子场关联转移到局域探测器，但这些关联能否改变稳定的策略行为尚不明确。作者试图在量子输入博弈框架下建立场关联与博弈均衡结构之间的定量联系。

Method: 构造量子输入博弈：两探测器从自由无质量标量场真空采集纠缠；裁判通过独立问题比特与相位校准的局域Bell测量将量子态映射为支付；在微扰最低阶计算平均收益，并分析允许退出或选择早/晚相互作用时的颤抖手完美均衡数目。

Result: 最低阶平均收益正比于带符号的纠缠余量 sqrt(H^2+Q^2)-P，其中H为Hadamard关联贡献、Q为Pauli-Jordan因果贡献、P为局域激发噪声。非退化支付下均衡数为1+2n_+，n_+为正收益时序均衡数。固定协议下五个可解析验证设置实现7→3→1→7→1序列，对应类空区、第一对易子波瓣、中心载波抵消、第二波瓣与波前后Huygens区。

Conclusion: 在固定量子输入验证协议内，不同场关联区域可生成不同的稳定策略结构，说明纠缠采集的场关联能够改变博弈的均衡形态。

Abstract: Entanglement harvesting transfers quantum field correlations to localized probes, yet whether these correlations can alter stable strategic behavior has remained unclear. We formulate a quantum-input game in which two Unruh-DeWitt detectors harvest vacuum entanglement from a free massless scalar field in $(3+1)$-dimensional Minkowski spacetime. A referee converts this state into payoffs using independent question qubits and phase-calibrated local Bell measurements. To leading perturbative order, the mean reward is proportional to the signed entanglement margin $\sqrt{\mathcal{H}^2+\mathcal{Q}^2}-\mathcal{P}$, where $\mathcal{H}$ is the Hadamard correlation contribution, $\mathcal{Q}$ is the Pauli-Jordan causal contribution, and $\mathcal{P}$ is the local excitation noise. Away from payoff degeneracies, allowing each player to exit or to choose an early or late interaction yields $1+2n_+$ trembling-hand perfect equilibria, where $n_+$ counts the positive-reward timing equilibria. For one fixed protocol, five analytically certified settings realize the sequence $7\to3\to1\to7\to1$. They represent the spacelike region, the first commutator lobe, central carrier cancellation, the second lobe, and the post-wavefront Huygens regime. Thus, within this fixed quantum-input verification protocol, distinct field-correlation regimes generate different stable strategic structures.

</details>


### [113] [Occupation-selective photon-pair interactions in a flux-pumped SQUID resonator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33228)
