*L. N. Ferreira,D. Z. Rossatto,A. S. M. de Castro,G. D. de Moraes Neto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文识别并刻画了通量泵浦约瑟夫森电路中一种占据数条件配对环境相互作用：两个光子在同一信号模中产生或湮灭，同时另一谐波模的占据数保持不变，该“控制模”同时改变配对共振频率与配对跃迁强度，从而实现选择性跃迁、纠缠及非高斯态制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通量泵浦约瑟夫森电路是参数光子产生与多模玻色控制的通用平台，但既有研究多关注激发交换型的参数过程。作者希望刻画一种不交换激发、仅由占据数调节的非线性相互作用，以拓展玻色模式间可控耦合的物理机制。

Method: 从完整约瑟夫森余弦势出发，考虑两个谐波简正模参与同一驱动SQUID坐标；识别出简并配对过程（信号模双光子产生/湮灭、控制模占据数不变）。发展互补的含时Schrieffer–Wolff与Magnus有效理论，揭示直接与虚拟过程；并与对未展开双模电路哈密顿量进行数值收敛Floquet处理的结果进行基准对照。

Result: 该相互作用产生依赖控制模占据数的二维配对谱，可实现玻色Fock晶格中的选择性跃迁；当控制模处于相干叠加态时，条件动力学转化为信号–控制模纠缠与控制模依赖的非高斯态产生。观测层面的对比进一步表明，配对跃迁强度的变化无法仅由占据数依赖的共振位移来解释。物理起源上，对角约瑟夫森非线性移动配对共振，而非线性约瑟夫森矩阵元使避交叉间隙本身依赖控制模占据数。

Conclusion: 本文确立了一种玻色子控制的参数相互作用：一个谐波模的状态可同时调控另一模中非线性过程的谱位置与耦合强度，为多模玻色量子控制与量子态工程提供了新机制。

Abstract: Flux-pumped Josephson circuits provide a versatile platform for parametric photon generation and multimode bosonic control. We identify an occupation-conditioned pair interaction that arises when two harmonic normal modes participate in the same driven SQUID coordinate. We consider a degenerate pair process in which two photons are created or annihilated in a signal mode while the occupation of a second harmonic mode remains unchanged. Although it does not exchange excitations, this controller mode modifies both the resonance frequency and the strength of the pair transition. The two effects have different physical origins: diagonal Josephson nonlinearities shift the pair resonance, whereas the nonlinear Josephson matrix element makes the avoided-crossing gap itself depend on the controller occupation. Starting from the full Josephson cosine, we develop complementary time-dependent Schrieffer--Wolff and Magnus descriptions that expose the direct and virtual processes responsible for this interaction, and benchmark the resulting effective theory against a numerically converged Floquet treatment of the unexpanded two-mode circuit Hamiltonian. The occupation dependence produces a two-dimensional pair spectrum that enables selective transitions within the bosonic Fock lattice. When the controller is prepared in a coherent superposition, the same interaction converts this conditional dynamics into signal--controller entanglement and controller-dependent non-Gaussian state generation. Observable-level comparisons further show that the variation of the pair-transition strength cannot be reproduced by occupation-dependent resonance shifts alone. These results establish a boson-controlled parametric interaction in which the state of one harmonic mode controls both the spectral position and the coupling strength of a nonlinear process in another.

</details>


### [114] [NxM-Version Programming for Quantum Software: High-Level Components across Frameworks and Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33255)
*Neilson Carlos Leite Ramalho,Higor Amario de Souza,Anthony Accioly,Valter Vieira de Camargo,Marcos Lordello Chaim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: Quanifi 将 Qiskit、Cirq、Qrisp、pyQuil、PennyLane 等量子软件框架的高层例程封装为 Apache NiFi 数据流画布上的组件，以 OpenQASM 2.0 交换电路，实现跨框架与跨后端的 N×M 执行并发现真实缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件工程尚处于起步阶段，编写量子程序仍需直接操作量子门与量子比特，并依赖各框架（Qiskit、Cirq、Qrisp、pyQuil、PennyLane）各自的工具链与专有知识，导致跨框架与跨硬件平台的可移植性差、验证困难。

Method: 提出 Quanifi，将各框架高层例程封装为 NiFi 数据流组件，组件间以 OpenQASM 2.0 交换电路；利用同一算法的多框架实现与多执行引擎形成 N×M 执行矩阵，每条结果标注其实现与引擎，从而将结果分歧溯源到实现、引擎或实现-引擎对。

Result: 在 IBM、IQM、Quantum Inspire 三台量子计算机上以三种框架实现 Grover 算法，并在通过两条路径访问的同一 IQM 设备上运行三种加法器构建器矩阵；N×M 执行发现三个真实缺陷：Quantum Inspire 适配器的门集声明错误、Tuna-17 上表现为 Rx 角度取反的执行结果、以及 Open Quantum 对电路的修改（后两者返回错误答案且未报错）。三个缺陷均已报告，Open Quantum 提供方确认并修复了其 QASM 解析缺陷。

Conclusion: 将异构量子软件框架与执行引擎统一到数据流编排中，并以 OpenQASM 作为交换格式，可通过 N×M 交叉执行实现有效的差分测试与缺陷定位，为量子软件工程提供了可用的工程化验证手段。

Abstract: Quantum computing has in recent years evolved from a purely theoretical field to an active area in both academia and industry. As a result, Quantum Software Engineering has emerged as an area that aims to organize the process of building, testing, and running quantum software. However, writing quantum software still requires programming with quantum gates and qubits, as well as knowledge specific to quantum software frameworks such as Qiskit, Cirq, Qrisp, pyQuil and PennyLane, each of which reaches quantum hardware through its own toolchain. We present Quanifi, which packages the high-level routines of these frameworks as components on the Apache NiFi dataflow canvas. The components exchange circuits as OpenQASM 2.0, so a circuit built by one framework can be executed by another. This allows for two types of redundancy: several frameworks implement the same algorithm, and several engines (simulators or real hardware) execute the same circuit. We describe N$\times$M program execution, which crosses the two: a single NiFi flow runs each of the $N$ implementations on each of the $M$ engines, and because every result is labelled with its implementation and its engine, a disagreement can be linked to an implementation, to an engine, or to a single implementation-engine pair. We ran Grover's algorithm built by three frameworks on three quantum computers from IBM, IQM and Quantum Inspire, and a matrix of three adder builders on one IQM device reached through two routes. N$\times$M execution helped us discover three real defects: an incorrect gate-set declaration in the Quantum Inspire adapter, execution consistent with negated $R_x$ angles on Tuna-17, and circuit modification through Open Quantum. The latter two returned wrong answers without reporting an error. We reported all three defects and Open Quantum's provider confirmed and fixed its QASM parsing defect.

</details>


### [115] [Preparation-controlled relaxation in an overdamped RLC circuit: A pedagogical route to the spectral Mpemba effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33277)
*Matheus H. dos Santos,Cler T. Garcez,Jeveson C. da Silva,G. D. de Moraes Neto,Norton G. de Almeida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文以过阻尼RLC电路为经典、可实验的谱Mpemba类比系统，从确定性、随机与开放系统三个层面揭示能量序反转机制：高能态可因慢/快模态激发权重不同而跨越至低能态之下；并在实验上验证，同时桥接到量子谐振子与参量振子的Mpemba行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回应一个反直觉问题——储能更多的系统能否冷却更快，即Mpemba效应。作者希望借助经典电路这一可控、易实验的平台，厘清能量序反转的物理根源（初始能量并不能唯一决定弛豫路径），并将该机制从确定性动力学推广到随机、开放系统乃至量子动力学，以统一理解经典与量子Mpemba效应。

Method: 1) 对过阻尼RLC电路建立确定性模型，推导模态制备条件与交叉判据，并用几何方法解释等能态弛豫差异；2) 实验上由示波器轨迹重构电荷、电流与能量进行验证；3) 引入外部白噪声驱动，考察反转的鲁棒性；4) 用平衡Ornstein-Uhlenbeck处理，区分系综均值携带的信息与协方差携带的信息（居中Gibbs制备保持均值能量序，位移/各向异性高斯态可编码慢快分区）；5) 通过Fokker-Planck生成元桥接到马尔可夫量子动力学；6) 分析热阻尼量子谐振子与阈值下参量振子；7) 用Liouvillian框架统一描述。

Result: 经典电路：给出模态制备条件与交叉判据，实验证实能量反转，且在白噪声驱动下反转依然存在。随机/O-U：居中Gibbs制备保持均值能量序，位移或各向异性高斯态可实现反转。量子谐振子：热阻尼下平均裸能超额以单一指数衰减，有限平均占据数下禁止能量序反转；阈值下参量振子恢复慢/快正交分区，可通过位移与协方差调控交叉。

Conclusion: 观测到的弛豫（及Mpemba式反转）并非仅由系统或生成元性质决定，而是联合取决于三要素：生成元的谱、制备所激发的模态、以及可观测量所可见的模态。Liouvillian表述为经典与量子情形提供了统一解释框架。

Abstract: Can a system with more stored energy cool faster than one with less? An overdamped resistor--inductor--capacitor (RLC) circuit offers a classical, experimentally accessible spectral Mpemba analogue: both states lose energy monotonically, yet the initially higher-energy state can cross below the lower-energy one. The reason is that initial energy alone does not determine the relaxation path; initial charge and current set how strongly the slow and fast decay modes are excited. We develop this mechanism from deterministic to stochastic and open-system descriptions. For the classical circuit, we derive the modal preparation conditions and crossing criterion, explain geometrically why equal-energy states can relax differently, and confirm the effect experimentally by reconstructing charge, current, and energy from oscilloscope traces. The inversion survives when the circuit is driven by externally injected white noise. An equilibrium Ornstein--Uhlenbeck treatment separates information carried by the ensemble mean from that stored in its covariance: centered Gibbs preparations preserve mean-energy ordering, while displaced or anisotropic Gaussian states can encode distinct slow and fast sectors. A Fokker--Planck generator then bridges to Markovian quantum dynamics. For a thermally damped quantum harmonic oscillator, the mean bare-energy excess decays with a single exponential factor, forbidding energy-order inversion for finite mean occupation. A below-threshold parametric oscillator restores slow and fast quadrature sectors, allowing crossings controlled by displacement and covariance. The Liouvillian formulation shows that observed relaxation depends jointly on the generator spectrum, the modes populated by preparation, and the modes visible to the observable.

</details>


### [116] [Local Many Worlds in Spacetime: Deutsch-Hayden Descriptors and Local Wavefunctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33453)
*Mordecai Waegell*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出"局域多世界"(local many worlds)量子力学形式，基于局域波函数与局域幺正演化、无波函数坍缩，并证明任意N-qubit系统的局域波函数总可由Deutsch-Hayden (DH)描述符直接构造，从而统一局域薛定谔图象与局域海森堡图象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝尔定理表明，任何局域且空间可分离、无非决定论与无回溯因果的物理理论必然是"多局域世界"理论。Everett多世界解释依赖组态空间中不可分离的普适波函数，缺乏透明的时空可分离性；而局域海森堡图象虽以DH描述符紧凑地编码信息，却因形式晦涩而难以理解和使用。作者希望构建并沟通两种图象，兼取其直观性与紧凑性。

Method: 采用局域多世界形式：每个时空事件拥有自己的局域波函数，仅由其过去光锥内的局域波函数与局域幺正演化算符构建，与类空间隔的局域波函数完全断开，即"局域薛定谔图象"；同时用赋予各系统的Deutsch-Hayden描述符在海森堡图象下刻画演化。两者均记录过去相互作用，作者给出由DH描述符直接构造任意N-qubit系统局域波函数的构造方法。

Result: 证明了局域波函数与DH描述符包含完全相同的信息：任意N-qubit系统的局域波函数可始终由DH描述符直接构造出来。这打通了局域海森堡图象与局域薛定谔图象之间的桥梁。

Conclusion: 局域多世界形式是时空中的透明可分离理论，具有洛伦兹协变因果结构，仅靠局域幺正演化守恒概率流、无坍缩。通过从DH描述符重构局域波函数，可同时获得薛定谔图象的直观物理叙述与海森堡图象的紧凑表示，提升局域海森堡图象的可及性。

Abstract: Bell's theorem establishes that a local and spatially separable physical theory with no superdeterminism or retrocausality must be a theory of many local worlds. The local many worlds formalism of quantum mechanics is a transparent separable physical theory in spacetime with a Lorentz covariant causal structure. Unlike the Everett many worlds interpretation, there is no nonseparable universal wavefunction in configuration space, but like Everett, there is only local unitary evolution which conserves probability (fluid) current, and no wavefunction collapse. Each event in spacetime has its own local wavefunction which is built up using only the local wavefunctions and local unitary evolution operators within its past light cone, and which is entirely disconnected from spacelike separated local wavefunctions. We call this the local Schrödinger picture. Local many worlds can also be described in the Heisenberg picture by ascribing Deutsch-Hayden (DH) descriptors to each individual system, each of which evolves under local unitary operations. Both the local wavefunctions and DH descriptors contain records of past interactions, and while the local wavefunctions provide a clear and intuitive physical narrative, the descriptors contain all of the same information in a much more compact form. Unfortunately, the compact form is also somewhat cryptic, which has made the local Heisenberg picture relatively inaccessible. The purpose of this article is to show that the local wavefunctions for any $N$-qubit system can always be constructed directly from the DH descriptors, allowing us to combine the benefits of both pictures.

</details>


### [117] [How many photons can a molecule scatter before its coherence is lost?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33475)
*M. R. Tarbutt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文计算了保守光阱中分子量子比特因散射囚禁激光光子而产生的退相干速率，并将结果用分子极化率的矢量和张量部分表示。虽然分子的退相干速率通常远大于原子，但在极化率矢量和张量部分均较小的波长处会被抑制。作者针对CaF分子确定了这些波长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子量子比特被囚禁在保守光阱中时，会因散射囚禁激光光子而损失相干性。因此需要定量理解并计算这种退相干速率。

Method: 将退相干速率分解为两部分：一部分等于两个量子比特态的平均拉曼散射速率，另一部分正比于两者瑞利散射振幅之差的平方。用分子极化率的矢量和张量部分来表示这一速率。

Result: 得到了以极化率矢量和张量部分表达的退相干速率。发现分子的退相干速率通常远大于原子，但在极化率矢量和张量部分均较小的波长处会被显著抑制。

Conclusion: 分子的退相干速率一般比原子大得多，但可通过选择极化率矢量和张量部分都较小的波长来抑制。作者针对CaF分子识别出了这些波长。

Abstract: Molecular qubits held in conservative optical traps may lose coherence when they scatter photons from the trapping lasers. The rate for this process has two parts, one equal to the mean Raman scattering rate of the two qubit states, and the other proportional to the square of the difference in their Rayleigh scattering amplitudes. This paper calculates the decoherence rate, expressing results in terms of the vector and tensor parts of the molecular polarizability, $α_1$ and $α_2$. While the decoherence rate is generally much larger for molecules than for atoms, it is suppressed at wavelengths where $α_1$ and $α_2$ are both small. The wavelengths where this occurs are identified for CaF molecules.

</details>


### [118] [The Fully Depolarizing Noise Conjecture for Entangled Physical States: A Twenty-Year Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33508)
*Gil Kalai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文重访作者2006年提出的"纠缠物理量子比特相关误差"猜想，该猜想认为任何量子计算机实现中，作用于纠缠物理量子比特的有效噪声信道都包含联合全去极化分量，其速率与双量子比特门误差相当，若成立将对可扩展量子容错构成重大挑战。猜想至今未决，但近期实验进展使其具备在当前设备上检验的可能。文章还讨论噪声敏感性与计算复杂性在NISQ系统中的作用、量子优势实验声明的统计分析两个相关方向，并包含作者与Yuri Gurevich学术与个人交往的回顾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在重新审视其2006年提出的关于纠缠物理量子比特相关误差的猜想，探讨该结构性约束是否超越标准噪声模型、是否对可扩展量子容错构成根本障碍；同时鉴于近期实验量子计算进展，认为该猜想已进入可实证检验的阶段，因而有必要系统梳理其意义与检验路径。

Method: 这是一篇回顾性与评论性论文，主要方法为理论分析与概念论证：重述2006年猜想的表述（有效噪声信道含联合全去极化分量，速率与双量子比特门误差相当），分析其相对标准噪声模型的超越之处及其对容错的潜在影响；并结合近期实验量子计算进展讨论可检验性。此外还涉及NISQ系统中噪声敏感性与计算复杂性的理论探讨，以及对量子优势实验声明的统计分析方法讨论。

Result: 论文未给出猜想的证明或反证，该猜想目前仍为开放问题；主要结果是论证该猜想在当前实验量子计算平台上已具备实证检验的可行性，并指出其对可扩展量子容错的潜在严重挑战。相关方向方面，讨论了噪声敏感性与计算复杂性在NISQ系统中的作用，以及针对量子优势实验声明的统计分析方法。

Conclusion: 作者认为，纠缠物理量子比特的相关误差猜想虽仍开放，但随着实验量子计算的发展已可被检验，若成立将严重挑战可扩展量子容错。同时强调需审慎评估NISQ系统的噪声敏感性与复杂性，并以更严格的统计分析审视量子优势的实验声明。文末还回顾了作者与Yuri Gurevich交织的学术与人生历程。

Abstract: In this paper I revisit my 2006 conjecture on correlated errors in entangled physical qubits, originally proposed as a potential obstruction to quantum fault tolerance. The conjecture asserts that, in any physical implementation of a quantum computer, the effective noise channel acting on entangled physical qubits contains a joint fully depolarizing component, with a rate comparable to that of two-qubit gate errors. This hypothesized structural constraint goes beyond standard noise models and, if valid, would pose a significant challenge to scalable quantum fault tolerance.
  The conjecture remains open, but recent advances in experimental quantum computing bring it within reach of empirical testing on current devices. I also discuss two related directions in my critical study of quantum computation: the role of noise sensitivity and computational complexity in noisy intermediate-scale quantum systems, and the statistical analysis of experimental claims of quantum advantage. Finally, since this paper is written for a volume honoring Yuri Gurevich, I include some reflections on the ways in which my scientific and personal trajectory became intertwined with Yuri's.

</details>


### [119] [Terminal-Register Certification for Finite-Measurement Learning of Multiscale Quantum States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33567)
*Bhvain Makwana,Kashyap Patel,Manjunath Joshi,Jaideep Mulherkar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过逆向二值MERA研究一维纯态量子态学习，提出噪声感知的有限采样认证定理，并在横场Ising基态上验证了高保真度与鲁棒性，同时比较MERA与局域电路的架构公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构化量子态学习不仅依赖表达能力强的拟设，还需要在有限测量和不完美实现下仍然有意义的可操作认证。作者希望解决有限采样与噪声条件下量子态学习协议的可认证性与架构比较公平性问题。

Method: 采用逆向二值多尺度纠缠重整化拟设（MERA）学习一维纯态；粗粒化过程中移除的量子比特被相干控制并在终端寄存器一起测量；比较理想顺序与终端测量调度；引入噪声感知定理，将个体校准的总变差实现预算纳入有限采样认证；在开边界横场Ising基态上估计协议，并进行固定电路鲁棒性验证与n=16的架构公平性研究（精确梯度多起点诊断、因果锥完整且参数匹配的局域电路对比）。

Result: 冻结8量子比特调度每轮使用5.6亿次模拟训练测量，在全部60次留出运行中保真度均高于0.99，平均保真度0.996886；覆盖1080个电路噪声单元和6480行置信覆盖，未出现锁定的可靠性违规。在n=16的120次精确梯度多起点诊断中，MERA在58/60配对重启中保真度更高，在60/60中长程误差更低，但没有运行满足预设平稳性准则。因果锥完整、参数匹配的局域电路总门暴露高2.62倍，却在保真度、长程误差、能量和熵的全部30次配对比较中落败。

Conclusion: 逆向二值MERA结合噪声感知有限采样认证，可在有限测量和电路噪声下实现高保真量子态学习与稳健认证；在架构层面，MERA相较参数匹配的局域电路在保真度、长程误差、能量与熵等指标上具有明显优势。

Abstract: Structured quantum-state learning not only depends on an expressive ansatz but also on an operational certificate that stays meaningful with finite measurements and imperfect implementation. We study pure one dimensional states learning by an inverse binary multiscale entanglement renormalization ansatz (MERA). In the learning procedure, the qubits removed during coarse graining are controlled coherently and measured together at the terminal register. We confirm that an ideal sequential and terminal measurement schedule delivers the same complete bit string distribution under matched causal operations, while normalized postselection can amplify perturbations inversely with prefix acceptance. A noise aware theorem introduces an individual calibrated total variation implementation budget to the finite shot certificate. The protocol is estimated on an open boundary transverse field Ising ground state. A frozen 8-qubit schedule using $560$ million simulated training measurements per run achieves fidelity above $0.99$ in all $60$ held-out runs, with a mean fidelity of $0.996886$. 1080 circuit-noise cells and 6480 confidence-coverage rows are covered by fixed-circuit robustness validation without a locked soundness violation. We then address architectural fairness at $n=16$ using three new studies. In a 120-run exact-gradient multistart diagnostic, MERA has higher fidelity in 58/60 paired restarts and lower long-range error in 60/60, although no run met the prespecified stationarity criterion. Finally, a causal cone-complete, parameter matched local circuit achieves $2.62\times$ greater aggregate gate exposure yet loses all 30 paired comparisons in fidelity, long-range error, energy, and entropy.

</details>


### [120] [Simultaneously Query-Optimal Quantum Linear-System Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33686)
*Minbo Gao,Zhengfeng Ji,Chenghua Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了量子线性系统算法可以同时达到矩阵预言机与右端项预言机的最优查询复杂度：在给定s的常数因子估计下，以O(κlog(1/ε))次U_A查询和O(κ/s)次U_b查询制备归一化解，并给出同一实例的最坏情况联合下界，表明两界可同时优化且联合最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子线性系统算法分别通过块编码预言机U_A访问系数矩阵、通过态制备预言机U_b访问右端项。此前已知两者的最优查询复杂度分别为O(κlog(1/ε))和O(κ/s)（其中s=α‖A^{-1}b‖），但能否由单一算法同时达到这两个界一直悬而未决，本文旨在解决这一开放问题。

Method: 给定s的常数因子估计，设计了一个量子线性系统算法，将态制备与精度细化分离：先完成与精度无关的态制备，再进行精度细化，从而使得只有矩阵查询次数依赖于目标精度ε。

Result: 算法以误差ε制备归一化解，同时使用O(κlog(1/ε))次U_A查询和O(κ/s)次U_b查询；并证明了匹配的同一实例最坏情况下界：对任意量子线性系统算法及任意解范数尺度，均存在一个Hermitian实例，使该算法同时需要Ω(κlog(1/ε))次矩阵查询和Ω(κ/s)次态制备查询。

Conclusion: 两类预言机的复杂度可以同时优化，所得到的查询复杂度界是联合最优的，从而正面回答了此前的开放问题。

Abstract: Quantum linear-system algorithms access the coefficient matrix through a block-encoding oracle $U_A$ and the right-hand side through a state-preparation oracle $U_b$. The optimal query complexities of these two oracles are known separately: $O(κ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ queries to $U_A$ and $O(κ/s)$ queries to $U_b$, where $s=α\|A^{-1}b\|$. Whether a single algorithm can achieve both bounds simultaneously has remained open.
  We resolve this question affirmatively. Given a constant-factor estimate of $s$, we give a quantum linear-system algorithm that prepares the normalized solution to error $\varepsilon$ using simultaneously $ O\left(κ\log\frac1\varepsilon\right)$ queries to $U_A$ and $O\left(κ/{s}\right)$ queries to $U_b$. The algorithm separates state preparation from precision refinement, so only the matrix-query count depends on the target accuracy.
  We also prove a matching same-instance worst-case lower bound: for every quantum linear-system algorithm and every solution-norm scale, there exists a single Hermitian instance on which that algorithm requires both $Ω(κ\log(1/\varepsilon))$ matrix queries and $Ω(κ/s)$ state-preparation queries. Thus the two oracle complexities can be optimized simultaneously, and the resulting bounds are jointly optimal.

</details>


### [121] [On Embedding Design in Quantum Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33700)
*Ban Q. Tran,Nahid Binandeh Dehaghani,Susan Mengel,Rafal Wisniewski,A. Pedro Aguiar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的量子嵌入框架，将坐标到量子态的映射视为塑造变分电路可用特征表示的函数变换，并在此基础上提出LQNN-TE-QPINN与AdaFreq-QPINN两种嵌入方式，在一维与二维Burgers方程上验证嵌入设计对QPINN近似与优化行为的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子物理信息神经网络（QPINN）通过以物理残差为损失训练参数化量子电路来求解PDE，但将坐标映射为量子态的嵌入环节作用仍未得到充分理解。本文旨在系统刻画嵌入设计对QPINN性能的影响。

Method: 提出统一嵌入框架，把嵌入形式化为决定变分电路可用特征表示的函数变换，并给出两种具体嵌入：(1) LQNN-TE-QPINN，用辅助量子电路生成数据依赖特征，并以相加而非相乘的方式与输入坐标结合；(2) AdaFreq-QPINN，将arccos-Chebyshev编码的频率缩放因子设为可训练参数。在一维和二维Burgers方程上，与直接编码、固定解析频率编码及替代混合架构进行对比，并测试模拟硬件噪声下的表现。

Result: 一维情形下，LQNN-TE-QPINN以720个可训练参数取得0.0916的相对L2误差，优于直接编码QPINN的0.4501以及参数约多11倍的局部自适应经典PINN的0.1233；AdaFreq-QPINN仅增加4个参数即取得解析嵌入中最低误差。二维情形下LQNN-TE-QPINN训练目标最低，但解误差与数据重上传QPINN未明显区分。模拟硬件噪声下，LQNN-TE-QPINN保持最低绝对导数误差，但相对无噪声基线退化最明显。

Conclusion: 嵌入设计对QPINN的近似能力与优化行为具有实质性影响，统一嵌入框架与所提嵌入方法为量子PDE求解器的设计提供了有效方向。

Abstract: Quantum physics-informed neural networks (QPINNs) solve partial differential equations (PDEs) by training parameterized quantum circuits against physics-based residuals, yet the role of the embedding that maps coordinates into quantum states remains insufficiently understood. In this research, we introduce a unified embedding framework that formulates embedding as a functional transformation shaping the feature representation available to the variational circuit, and we propose two embeddings within it. The Linear Quantum Neural Network Trainable Embedding QPINN (LQNN-TE-QPINN) generates data-dependent features with an auxiliary quantum circuit and adds them to the input coordinates, instead of multiplying them as in our previous formulation. The adaptive-frequency QPINN (AdaFreq-QPINN) promotes the frequency-scaling factors of an arccos-Chebyshev encoding to trainable parameters. We evaluate both against direct and fixed analytical frequency encodings and alternative hybrid architectures on one- and two-dimensional Burgers equations. In one dimension, LQNN-TE-QPINN achieves a relative $L_2$ error of 0.0916 with 720 trainable parameters, compared with 0.4501 for direct-encoding QPINN and 0.1233 for a locally adaptive classical physics-informed neural network with approximately eleven times more parameters, while AdaFreq-QPINN attains the lowest error among the analytical embeddings with only four additional parameters. In two dimensions, LQNN-TE-QPINN attains the lowest training objective, although its solution error is not clearly separated from that of a data re-uploading QPINN. Under simulated hardware noise, LQNN-TE-QPINN retains the lowest absolute derivative error while degrading most relative to its noiseless baseline. These results demonstrate that embedding design substantially influences the approximation and optimization behavior of QPINNs.

</details>


### [122] [Quantum data processing equality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33815)
*Tai-Hsuan Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过复条带上的边界与曲面Poisson积分表示，将量子数据处理不等式推广为精确等式，并给出相对熵损失与BS损失的分解及恢复界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子相对熵在量子信道下单调不增（量子数据处理不等式），但损失的物理机制与恢复重构的定量刻画尚不精确，且已有恢复界对细小特征值存在逆幂依赖，需要更优的刻画。

Method: 利用复条带上的边界与曲面Poisson积分表示，将相对熵损失分解为Petz恢复分量与相对模动力学失配的边界项；进一步表示为原态与其平均Petz恢复态之间的测量相对熵加上非负变分余项；对BS损失建立类似积分表示，分解为负对数Uhlmann保真度加平均重构与非负变分余项。

Result: 得到了相对熵损失与BS损失的精确分解；在任意指定模参数（含标准未旋转Petz映射）下建立恢复界，相对熵损失具最优平方根标度；前置因子依赖不同特征值计数或对数谱量，优于以往对细小特征值的逆幂依赖。

Conclusion: 该工作将量子数据处理不等式从不等式提升为精确等式，揭示损失的两类非负机制，并给出具最优标度与更优谱依赖的恢复界。

Abstract: The quantum data processing inequality asserts that quantum relative entropy is monotonically non-increasing under quantum channels. Here, we promote this fundamental inequality to an exact equality via boundary and surface Poisson integral representations across a complex strip. The relative-entropy loss is resolved into two independent, nonnegative physical mechanisms: an operational Petz recovery component governing state reconstructibility, and a boundary term measuring the mismatch of relative modular dynamics. We further express the loss as the measured relative entropy between the original state and its averaged Petz recovery state plus a nonnegative variational remainder. For the Belavkin--Staszewski (BS) loss, analogous integral representations yield a decomposition into negative log Uhlmann fidelity with an averaged reconstruction plus a nonnegative variational remainder. Furthermore, we establish recovery bounds at any prescribed modular parameter, including the canonical unrotated Petz map, with optimal square-root scaling in the relative entropy loss. The prefactors depend on distinct-eigenvalue counts or logarithmic spectral quantities, improving upon the inverse-power dependence on small eigenvalues in previous bounds.

</details>


### [123] [Dynamic Sculpting of Photon Statistics in a Raman-Coupled Cavity QED System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33837)
*Amir Rahmani,Alireza Dehghani*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过拉曼耦合腔-原子系统加参量放大，周期调制失谐可在时间上主动切换光子聚束与反聚束，实现连续波下的时间门控反聚束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳态光子统计已被广泛研究，但在时间上动态操控光子统计性质仍具挑战；本文旨在实现聚束与反聚束区间之间的主动、动态控制。

Method: 基于拉曼耦合的腔-原子系统并引入参量放大，对中间态失谐进行时间调制以加深光子阻塞或增强聚束；同时调制腔失谐与中间态失谐以产生静态或单一调制无法获得的反聚束。

Result: 时间调制中间态失谐可分别加深光子阻塞或增强光子聚束；同时调制腔与中间态失谐可产生静态条件下无法实现的反聚束；周期性调制使反聚束在固定时间间隔重现，从而在连续波运行下以调制频率决定的重复率实现时间门控反聚束。

Conclusion: 提出并验证了一种基于拉曼辅助量子干涉的通用机制，可用于动态工程化光子关联，为动态调控光子统计提供新途径。

Abstract: Controlling photon statistics is pivotal to many applications in classical and quantum photonics. While steady-state photon statistics have been widely studied, dynamically manipulating these statistical properties in time remains elusive. Herein, we propose a scheme to actively control photon statistics between bunching and antibunching regimes through quantum interference. Our approach relies on a Raman-coupled cavity-atom system with parametric amplification, where time modulating the detuning of the intermediate state allows us to either deepen the photon blockade or enhance photon bunching. Simultaneous modulation of the cavity and intermediate-state detunings produces antibunching that cannot be achieved under static conditions or by modulating either detuning alone. Because the modulation is periodic, the antibunching reappears at regular time intervals, enabling time-gated photon antibunching under continuous-wave operation at a repetition rate determined by the modulation frequency. These results show a versatile mechanism based on Raman-assisted quantum interference for dynamically engineering photon correlations.

</details>


### [124] [Nonlinear engineering of Hong-Ou-Mandel interference with structured light](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33862)
*Simon Darveau,Bienvenu Ndagano*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过调控泵浦场的宇称（旋转结构光泵浦模式），可控制Hong-Ou-Mandel干涉中光子对聚束与反聚束的转换，并在高阶Hermite-Gaussian模式和携带轨道角动量的Laguerre-Gaussian模式下实现灵敏度与角分辨率增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子对进入Hong-Ou-Mandel干涉仪时，其聚束或反聚束行为取决于被测自由度中量子态的对称性，从而产生相长或相消干涉。对于自发参量下转换产生的光子对，这一对称性可通过线性变换与投影操作调控，也可通过裁剪泵浦场实现。已有研究多采用前者，而泵浦场本身特性（尤其是宇称）对干涉转换行为的影响尚缺乏系统研究，本文即针对此空白展开。

Method: 采用裁剪泵浦场的方式，从理论与实验两方面研究泵浦宇称对相长-相消干涉转换的形状与频率的影响。实验中使用旋转的结构化泵浦模式，分别编码于Hermite-Gaussian与Laguerre-Gaussian基下，并观测干涉转换行为随模式阶数或轨道角动量含量的变化。

Result: 在Hermite-Gaussian基情形，转换呈现出该模式族特有的丰富结构，且随模式阶数增加灵敏度增强；在Laguerre-Gaussian基情形，角分辨率获得与轨道角动量含量成正比的整数倍增强，效果类似于NOON态。

Conclusion: 泵浦场的宇称可有效调控Hong-Ou-Mandel干涉的转换特性，结构化泵浦模式为量子干涉提供了新的调控维度，并开启了Hong-Ou-Mandel增强型旋转传感的可能性。

Abstract: A pair of photons entering a Hong-Ou-Mandel interferometer will bunch or anti-bunch depending on the symmetry of their state in the measured degree of freedom, resulting in constructive or destructive interference. For photon pairs generated through spontaneous parametric down-conversion, this symmetry can be engineered through linear transformations and projections applied to the photon pairs, or by tailoring the pump field. Here, we adopt the latter approach to demonstrate that the pump's parity affects the shape and frequency of the transition from constructive to destructive interference. We demonstrate this concept theoretically and experimentally using a rotating structured pump mode encoded in the Hermite-Gaussian and Laguerre-Gaussian bases. In the former case, we show that the transition exhibits a rich structure unique to the mode family, resulting in enhanced sensitivity as the mode order increases. In the latter case, we demonstrate, as with NOON states, an integer enhancement in angular resolution by a factor proportional to the orbital angular momentum content, thereby opening the possibility of Hong-Ou-Mandel-enhanced rotation sensing.

</details>


### [125] [Quantum-Computing Self-Consistent Kohn-Sham DFT: Plane-Wave-Orthonormalized Orbitals with a Dual-Basis Quantum Eigensolver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33934)
*Lazaro Calderin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向量子计算机的第一量子化、全电子、全势 Kohn-Sham 密度泛函理论方法，结合经典优化的量子线路本征求解器，在 PWOO 基组下实现自洽场求解，并在 H8 链及离子阱硬件上验证了精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将全电子、全势 Kohn-Sham DFT 引入量子计算，需解决任意正交归一基下 SCF 本征问题在量子硬件上的高效求解与基组表示问题。

Method: 采用第一量子化全电子全势 KS-DFT；构建平面波正交归一轨道（PWOO）基（平面波 + 与其正交归一的原子轨道）；使用经典优化的量子线路本征求解器，具双基形式：全基自举后，每步 SCF 在 K 个函数的收缩基（⌈log2 K⌉ 量子比特）中求解，当外部残差超阈值时在全基中更新。

Result: H8 链（SVWN，K=8）：态矢量模拟 SCF 与经典计算在 32768 个平面波及至 cc-pVTZ 的 PWOO 基下吻合于 0.02 mHa 内；收缩使 PWOO 能量变化不超过 5.7 μHa。含模拟散粒噪声时，STO-3G PWOO SCF 收敛至接近无噪声不动点。在 IonQ Forte-1 上，三量子比特寄存器读出在 1 个标准差内复现无噪声 PWOO-AO 能量差。

Conclusion: 该双基量子线路本征求解方案可在量子计算机上实现高精度全电子 KS-DFT，收缩基近似误差极小，并在噪声模拟与真实离子阱硬件上得到验证。

Abstract: We present a first-quantization, all-electron, full-potential Kohn-Sham density functional theory method for quantum computers, based on a classically optimized quantum-circuit eigensolver for the self-consistent field (SCF) eigenproblems in any orthonormal basis. The plane-wave-orthonormalized-orbital (PWOO) basis combines plane waves with atomic orbitals (AOs) orthonormalized against them and one another. The eigensolver has a dual-basis form: after full-basis bootstrap steps, it solves each SCF step in a contracted basis of $K$ functions ($\lceil\log_2 K\rceil$ qubits), updated in the full basis when the residual outside it exceeds a threshold. For an H$_8$ chain (SVWN, $K=8$), the state-vector-emulated SCF agrees with classical calculations within 0.02 mHa for 32768 plane waves and PWOO bases up to cc-pVTZ; contraction changes these PWOO energies by at most 5.7 $μ$Ha. With emulated shot noise, the STO-3G PWOO SCF converges near the noise-free fixed point. On IonQ Forte-1, three-qubit-register readout reproduces the noise-free PWOO-AO energy difference within one standard deviation.

</details>


### [126] [Hardness of Approximating Quantum Code Distance Beyond $\sqrt{N}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33956)
*Upendra Kapshikar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究稳定子码最小距离近似问题的计算复杂性，填补了量子码领域中加性间隙可达O(√N)与经典NP-hard线性加性间隙之间的空隙，并首次开展细粒度复杂性研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典最小距离问题即使在线性加性间隙下近似也是NP-hard的，但对于量子码，已有归约（Kapshikar-Kundu、Grigorescu-Jha-Samperton）只能达到块长N的O(√N)加性间隙，与经典结果之间存在显著空隙。作者旨在关闭这一空隙，并开辟该问题的细粒度研究新方向。

Method: 通过复杂性理论归约方法：一方面给出新的归约证明常数加性间隙αN下的不可近似性；另一方面在细粒度框架下建立与SETH及非均匀Gap-ETH的条件性下界。

Result: 证明：除非NP ⊆ coRP，否则不存在随机算法能以常数加性间隙αN（α>0）近似距离；对任意ε>0，除非SETH失败，否则不存在2^{(1-ε)κ}·poly(N)时间的随机算法计算含κ个逻辑量子比特的码的距离；除非非均匀Gap-ETH失败，否则不存在2^{o(N)}时间的随机算法在线性加性间隙下近似距离（块长、逻辑量子比特数与间隙同时线性）。

Conclusion: 该工作将量子稳定子码最小距离近似的不可近似性从O(√N)加性间隙强化至线性加性间隙，与经典结果相匹配，并在细粒度层面给出基于SETH和Gap-ETH的紧致条件下界。

Abstract: We study the computational complexity of approximating the minimum distance of a stabilizer code. Classically, the minimum distance problem is NP-hard to approximate even to within an additive gap linear in the block length. For quantum codes, however, the reductions of Kapshikar and Kundu and of Grigorescu, Jha and Samperton reach only an additive $O(\sqrt{N})$ gap in the block length $N$. We close this gap and, in a separate direction, initiate the fine-grained study of the problem.
  We show that no randomized algorithm approximates the distance to within an additive $αN$, for a constant $α> 0$, unless $\mathsf{NP} \subseteq \mathsf{coRP}$. In the fine-grained setting, for every $\varepsilon > 0$, no randomized $2^{(1-\varepsilon)κ}\,\mathrm{poly}(N)$-time algorithm computes the distance of a code with $κ$ logical qubits unless SETH falls; and no randomized $2^{o(N)}$-time algorithm approximates the distance to within a linear additive gap, on instances where block length, number of logical qubits, and gap are simultaneously linear, unless non-uniform Gap-ETH falls.

</details>


### [127] [QBX: A Compiler for 2-local Qubit Hamiltonian Simulation on Quantum Chiplets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33997)
*Zikun Li,Zhuoming Chen,Zhihao Jia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出QBX，首个面向量子chiplet架构的2-local量子比特哈密顿量模拟编译器，通过分层策略与highway机制高效降低跨chiplet通信开销，在电路深度与操作数上显著优于现有编译器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2-local量子比特哈密顿量模拟是量子计算的基础任务，应用广泛。现有通用chiplet编译器仅针对门级程序，缺乏哈密顿量模拟的高层语义优化；而领域专用哈密顿量模拟编译器面向单体架构，可扩展性有限且无法适应chiplet异构性，导致电路输出次优。

Method: QBX采用可扩展的分层方法，减少跨chiplet通信的数量与距离；集成面向chiplet的highway机制以实现高效长程量子比特通信，降低跨chiplet交互成本；并将2-local Pauli字符串高效聚合为多目标受控门，借助highway实现深度优化。

Result: 评估表明，QBX在电路深度与操作数量上明显优于通用编译器与领域专用编译器，同时可扩展到更大规模的2-local哈密顿量模拟。

Conclusion: QBX是首个面向chiplet架构的2-local哈密顿量模拟编译器，结合高层语义优化与chiplet通信优化，在性能与可扩展性上均取得显著优势。

Abstract: 2-local qubit Hamiltonian simulation, a fundamental task in quantum computing, is widely applied in various applications. This paper presents QBX, the first quantum compiler designed for 2-local qubit Hamiltonian simulation on quantum chiplet architectures. Existing general-purpose quantum compilers for chiplet architectures target quantum programs at the gate level and miss optimizations requiring high-level semantics of Hamiltonian simulation. Conversely, existing domain-specific compilers for Hamiltonian simulation are designed for monolithic architectures, and their limited scalability and inability to adapt to the heterogeneity of chiplet architectures lead to sub-optimal circuit outputs. QBX implements a scalable hierarchical approach to reduce both the amount and distance of cross-chiplet communications. It integrates the highway mechanism, a chiplet-oriented solution for efficient long-range qubit communication, to diminish cross-chiplet interaction costs. Furthermore, QBX efficiently aggregates 2-local Pauli strings into multi-target controlled gates, harnessing highways for deep optimization. Our evaluations show that QBX markedly outperforms both general-purpose and domain-specific compilers in circuit depth and operation count, while scaling to much larger 2-local Hamiltonian simulations.

</details>


### [128] [Nakayama's reduction of quantum topos and Bayesian quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34200)
*Atsuhide Mori*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将 Döring-Isham 拓扑量子理论中的语境限制与概率定义统一为对拓扑的单次选择，用以支持贝叶斯视角，并将概率随数据变化描述为预测理论适用性的局部位移，进而应用于量子计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nakayama 已给出限制 Döring-Isham 拓扑量子理论中语境的方法，并能将该限制与概率定义统一为单一拓扑选择。本文旨在阐明这种统一如何支持"概率随数据获取而变化"的贝叶斯观点。

Method: 以拓扑选择统一语境限制与概率定义；将概率变化刻画为预测理论适用性的局部位移（local shift）。

Result: 说明了该统一框架与贝叶斯概率更新视角的一致性，并给出其在量子计算中的应用。

Conclusion: 拓扑层面的统一为贝叶斯式概率更新提供了形式化基础，可视为预测理论适用性的局部位移，并在量子计算中具有应用价值。

Abstract: Nakayama found the way to limit the contexts in the Döring-Isham topos quantum theory and unified such limitation and the definition of probability as a single choice of topology. We explain how this unification supports the Bayesian perspective where the probability changes as data is obtained. We describe this change as a local shift of the applicability of a predictive theory and apply it to quantum computing.

</details>


### [129] [Quantum memory in an effective $Λ$-type system using dynamic detuning control of a superconducting qubit pair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34201)
*Hsin Chang,Kai-I Chu,Ite A. Yu,Yen-Hsiang Lin,Yung-Fu Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了由一对可调超导量子比特构成的等效Λ型系统中的量子光存储，通过调节比特失谐实现可控光谱响应、慢光效应以及按需存储与读取量子场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为构建可扩展超导量子网络，需要一种极简且可调的量子存储架构，实现量子场的长寿命存储与按需读取。

Method: 在半无限传输线上放置两个相隔半个波长的可调超导量子比特，形成等效Λ型系统；利用比特间频率失谐耦合超辐射激发态与亚辐射亚稳态，并通过动态调控失谐来控制光谱响应与光存储过程。

Result: 调节比特失谐可获得可控光谱响应并伴随慢光行为；动态控制失谐可将入射量子场映射到亚辐射集体态，实现长寿命激发存储并可按需读取。

Conclusion: 该体系可作为一种极简、可调的量子存储架构，适用于可扩展超导量子网络。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate quantum-light storage in an effective $Λ$-type system formed by a pair of tunable superconducting qubits coupled to a semi-infinite transmission line and separated by half a wavelength. The frequency detuning between the qubits directly couples the superradiant excited state and the subradiant metastable state. By adjusting the qubit detuning, the system exhibits a controllable spectral response accompanied by slow-light behavior. Dynamically controlling the detuning enables an incident quantum field to be mapped onto the subradiant collective state, stored as a long-lived excitation, and subsequently retrieved on demand. Our results establish the proposed system as a minimal and tunable quantum-memory architecture for scalable superconducting quantum networks.

</details>


### [130] [Monotonicity of the Rényi channel capacity under non-signaling assisted channel simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34213)
*Tina Shariat,Matt Hoogsteder-Riera,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究发送方与接收方共享的非信号关联能否提升经典信道的Rényi容量，证明任何非信号盒子均不能在任意阶（从零阶经Shannon容量到无穷阶极限）提高Rényi容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非信号盒子的类严格大于共享随机性辅助的编解码器对，因此基于独立预处理与后处理的传统论证不适用；且定义此类盒子的约束对盒子是线性的，而Rényi容量对信道是非线性的，二者存在结构错配，需要新方法解决。

Method: 采用基于Legendre变换的变分方法，将Rényi容量重写为一族仿射泛函上的极值，使信道在其中呈线性出现；再结合对Shannon容量和端点阶的单独论证，覆盖从零阶到无穷阶的全部阶数。

Result: 证明非信号盒子在任何阶均不能提升Rényi容量；由此推出非信号模拟不能在容量以下提高随机编码指数或球填充指数，也不能在容量以上降低强逆指数。

Conclusion: 非信号关联在Rényi容量及相应编码指数方面不带来超越经典信道本身的增益，明确了非信号资源在信道容量提升上的局限性。

Abstract: We ask whether non-signaling correlations shared between the sender and receiver can increase the Rényi capacity of a classical channel. Non-signaling boxes form a strictly larger class than that of encoder and decoder pairs assisted by shared randomness, so the usual argument based on independent preprocessing and postprocessing is insufficient, and the constraints defining such boxes are linear in the box, while the Rényi capacity depends on the channel nonlinearly. We resolve this mismatch with a variational approach based on the Legendre transform, which recasts the capacity as an extremum over a family of affine functionals in which the channel appears linearly. Combining this method with separate arguments for the Shannon capacity and endpoint orders, we prove that no non-signaling box can raise the Rényi capacity at any order, from order zero through the Shannon capacity to the limiting order at infinity. Consequently, non-signaling simulation cannot increase the random-coding or sphere-packing exponent below capacity, or decrease the strong-converse exponent above capacity.

</details>


### [131] [A threshold for maximal Schmidt number from spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34255)
*Junhyeong An,Soojoon Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过推导(d-1)-block positive算子的谱界，证明当p≤⌊d²/2⌋时LS_p⊆ASN_{d-1}，给出仅涉及最大特征值的充分条件，并应用于reduction criterion，同时构造反例表明全局酉不变条件的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schmidt数刻画两体量子态纠缠的维度。核心问题是：仅凭C^d⊗C^d上量子态的谱（特征值），何时能保证其Schmidt数非最大（即≤d-1）。作者希望建立谱条件与Schmidt数上界之间的联系，并推广已有关于绝对PPT态的结果。

Method: 推导(d-1)-block positive算子的谱界；定义LS_p为最大特征值不超过p个最小特征值之和的态集合，ASN_{d-1}为所有全局酉变换下Schmidt数均≤d-1的态集合；证明包含关系LS_p⊆ASN_{d-1}；并将结果应用于reduction criterion下的态。

Result: 证明LS_p⊆ASN_{d-1}对p≤⌊d²/2⌋成立，由此得到仅依赖最大特征值的简单充分条件；证明在任意全局酉变换下满足reduction criterion的态均属于ASN_{d-1}，该结论涵盖了绝对PPT态属于ASN_{d-1}的既有结果；并构造出对每个d≥3、在两子系统上均满足reduction criterion却具最大Schmidt数的态。

Conclusion: 谱条件可充分保证Schmidt数非最大；reduction criterion在全局酉不变意义下蕴含Schmidt数≤d-1，推广了绝对PPT态的结果；但全局酉不变要求是本质的，因为存在满足reduction criterion却具最大Schmidt数的反例态。

Abstract: The Schmidt number quantifies the dimensionality of entanglement in bipartite quantum states. We investigate when the spectrum of a state on $\mathbb{C}^d \otimes \mathbb{C}^d$ alone guarantees that its Schmidt number is not maximal. By deriving spectral bounds for $(d-1)$-block positive operators, we prove that $\mathrm{LS}_p\subseteq \mathrm{ASN}_{d-1}$ for $p\leq \lfloor d^2/2\rfloor$, where $\mathrm{LS}_p$ denotes the set of states whose largest eigenvalue does not exceed the sum of their $p$ smallest eigenvalues, and $\mathrm{ASN}_{d-1}$ denotes the set of states whose Schmidt number remains at most $d-1$ under all global unitaries. This also yields a simple sufficient condition involving only the largest eigenvalue. As an application, we show that states satisfying the reduction criterion under arbitrary global unitaries belong to $\mathrm{ASN}_{d-1}$ in every dimension. This subsumes the earlier result that absolutely positive partial transpose states belong to $\mathrm{ASN}_{d-1}$. The global unitary requirement is nevertheless essential: for every $d\geq3$, we construct states of maximal Schmidt number satisfying the reduction criterion on both subsystems.

</details>


### [132] [Optimal Quantum Algorithms for Ordered Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34293)
*Joseph Carolan,Andrew M. Childs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文解决了有序搜索问题的量子查询复杂度精确常数问题，给出了两个达到最优查询数 (1/π)ln n + o(log n) 的量子算法：一个零错误算法和一个精确算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典二分搜索需 ⌈log₂ n⌉ 次查询且最优；量子算法虽能提供常数倍加速，但最优常数长期未定，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过问题的连续松弛构造零错误算法；并基于 Farhi、Goldstone、Gutmann 与 Sipser 多项式规划的解析解构造精确算法。

Result: 提出两个量子算法，均使用最优的 (1/π)ln n + o(log n) 次查询。

Conclusion: 有序搜索的量子查询复杂度最优常数被确定为与 (1/π)ln n 一致的渐近量级，闭合了长期存在的常数间隙。

Abstract: Ordered search is the problem of locating a target element in a sorted list of size $n$ using comparison queries. Classically, binary search requires $\lceil \log_2 n\rceil$ queries, which is optimal. Quantum algorithms offer a constant-factor speedup, but the precise constant has been a longstanding open question. We close this gap by exhibiting two new quantum algorithms for ordered search, each using the optimal $\frac{1}π\ln n+o(\log n)$ queries. The first, discovered by Claude Fable 5, is a simple zero-error algorithm derived from a continuum relaxation of the problem. The second, discovered by GPT-5.6-Sol (informed by Claude's zero-error algorithm), is an exact algorithm based on an analytic solution of the polynomial program of Farhi, Goldstone, Gutmann, and Sipser.

</details>


### [133] [Observation of a topological edge state among localized bulk states in the anisotropic quantum Rabi model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34332)
*Sungjoo Lim,Chanyang Im,Christopher G. Yale,Brian K. McFarland,Edward C. Tortorici,Daniel S. Lobser,Melissa C. Revelle,Susan M. Clark,Mahn-Soo Choi,Junki Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在囚禁离子量子模拟器中研究各向异性量子Rabi模型（AQRM）的拓扑边缘态（TES）与本征态，发现TES具有明确的手性与近乎完全的spin-boson可分离性，其玻色子部分为压缩真空态（压缩达6.45 dB）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑相由离散对称性保护边界模式以抵抗局域扰动。当平移周期性缺失时，体态也变为局域化，导致拓扑边缘态无法仅通过空间局域性来区分。因此需要寻找能刻画TES的 intrinsic 拓扑特征。本工作旨在解决该问题并实现本征态层面的拓扑现象表征。

Method: 在一维合成晶格中，利用囚禁离子量子模拟器实现AQRM；通过随格点指数变化的非均匀耦合破坏平移对称性；测量TES与体本征态的空间分布；提取手性与spin-boson可分离性作为拓扑特征；并进行相空间层析。

Result: TES与体态均呈现局域分布，但TES展现出well-defined chirality和near-complete spin-boson separability，而体态不具备。相空间层析表明TES的玻色子分量为压缩真空态，压缩达6.45 dB。

Conclusion: 这些结果通过TES的内禀拓扑特征对其进行识别，并建立本征态层面表征作为探测拓扑现象的途径。

Abstract: Topological phases are governed by discrete symmetries that protect boundary modes against local perturbations. When translational periodicity is absent, the bulk states also become localized, so that a topological edge state can no longer be distinguished from them by spatial localization alone. Here, we investigate the topological edge state (TES) and bulk eigenstates of the anisotropic quantum Rabi model (AQRM) in a trapped-ion quantum simulator. The AQRM hosts a topological phase in a one-dimensional synthetic lattice, whose translational symmetry is broken by the non-uniform couplings scaling with the site index. While both the TES and bulk states show localized distributions, we find that the TES exhibits well-defined chirality and near-complete spin--boson separability as signatures of the topological phase, in contrast to the bulk states. Phase-space tomography further reveals that the bosonic component of the TES is a squeezed vacuum state, with squeezing up to 6.45 dB. These results identify the TES through its intrinsic topological signatures and establish eigenstate-level characterization as a route to probing topological phenomena.

</details>


### [134] [Cavity magnonics and bound states in the continuum with Bragg and anti-Bragg mirrors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34365)
*B. -Y. Wu,K. -P. Li,G. Chen,W. -M. Zhou,C. -X. Run,K. -M. Hsieh,A. F. Kockum,Z. -R. Lin,W. Nie,I. -C. Hoi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用双开放波导中两个周期性铁磁球阵列与一个探针球，实验实现了腔磁子学中的连续域束缚态（BIC），并区分了Bragg与反Bragg腔中不同的超模耦合行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性共振发射体阵列耦合到一维波导可充当反射镜（如Bragg镜），两个这样的镜子构成可承载BIC的有效腔；但生成并探测此类BIC的空间分布一直具有挑战性，因此本文试图在腔磁子学体系中实验实现并探测BIC。

Method: 在双开放波导结构中，采用两个周期性铁磁球阵列作为反射镜、一个探针球置于腔区；分别构建晶格常数不同的Bragg腔与反Bragg腔，并通过移动探针球对腔场进行位置依赖的探测。

Result: 在Bragg腔中存在由镜球构成的简并超模，单个暗超模与腔区探针磁子相干耦合，形成两个极化激元，其劈裂随探针球尺寸与镜球数量变化；反Bragg腔在带隙中具有亮、暗超模，显著改变磁子-腔相互作用；移动探针球实现了腔场的位置依赖探测，突显了BIC的作用。

Conclusion: 该具有扫描探针的灵活实验平台为探测光-物质相互作用产生的其他奇异态、与超导电路接口构建混合量子网络，以及研究Bragg与反Bragg腔中的非厄米物理提供了可能。

Abstract: A periodic resonant emitter array coupled to a one-dimensional waveguide can act as a mirror. A typical example is a Bragg mirror, where emitters are spaced by multiples of half wavelengths and collectively enhance light reflection. Two such mirrors form an effective cavity that hosts bound states in the continuum (BICs). However, generating and detecting the spatial profiles of such BICs has proven challenging. Here, we experimentally demonstrate BICs in cavity magnonics using two periodic ferrimagnetic-sphere arrays and a probe sphere in a dual-open-waveguide architecture. We realize both Bragg and anti-Bragg cavities, whose mirrors have different lattice constants. In the Bragg cavity, there are degenerate supermodes formed by mirror spheres. We show that a single dark supermode coherently couples to the probe magnon in the cavity region, forming two polaritons whose splitting scales with both the probe-sphere size and the number of mirror spheres. By contrast, the anti-Bragg cavity has bright and dark supermodes in a bandgap, substantially changing the magnon-cavity interaction. Moreover, by moving the probe sphere, we perform position-dependent detection of the cavity field, highlighting the role of BICs in these cavities. Our flexible experimental setup with a scanning probe opens possibilities to detect other exotic states created by light-matter interaction, to interface with superconducting circuits in hybrid quantum networks, and to study non-Hermitian physics with Bragg and anti-Bragg cavities.

</details>


### [135] [Experimental Realization and Phase-Space Winding of a Topological Defect State in the Quantum Rabi Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34389)
*Kyungmin Lee,Jiyong Kang,Sunkyu Yu,Jaehun You,Wonhyeong Choi,Taehyun Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在单个囚禁的171Yb+离子中实验实现了量子Rabi模型的拓扑缺陷态，并通过特征函数层析重构相空间分布，观测到绕数为1和0的Wigner质心轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将非均匀合成晶格中的拓扑缺陷态物理与混合自旋-玻色系统的可测相空间几何联系起来。

Method: 利用Fock态晶格表象中振子的内禀数依赖耦合形成可调界面；在各边带相位设置下独立制备缺陷态，并用特征函数层析重构运动相空间分布；辅以载波幅度调制作为动力学探测。

Result: Wigner质心轨迹在两种耦合区分别呈现绕数1和0；改变载波幅度可缩放轨迹但保持绕数不变；自旋响应与缺陷态的激发能隙及可及跃迁通道一致。

Conclusion: 实验将非均匀合成晶格中的拓扑缺陷态物理与混合自旋-玻色系统的相空间几何联系起来，为在量子Rabi模型中研究拓扑缺陷态提供了实验平台。

Abstract: We experimentally realize a topological defect state of the quantum Rabi model in a single trapped $^{171}\mathrm{Yb}^{+}$ ion. The state is supported by a tunable interface arising from the oscillator's intrinsic number-dependent coupling in the Fock-state-lattice representation. We independently prepare the state at each sideband-phase setting and reconstruct its motional phase-space distribution using characteristic-function tomography. The resulting Wigner-centroid trajectories exhibit winding numbers of one and zero in the two coupling regimes. Changing the carrier amplitude rescales the trajectories while preserving their windings. Carrier-amplitude modulation provides a complementary dynamical probe, yielding a spin response consistent with the excitation gaps and accessible transition channels from the defect state. The experiment connects topological defect-state physics in a nonuniform synthetic lattice to the measurable phase-space geometry of a hybrid spin-boson system.

</details>


### [136] [Coherence Rather Than Error Rate Governs Privacy in Multi-Tenant Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34411)
*Farhad Farokhi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于量子态可区分性的信息论泄漏度量，用于刻画多租户量子计算中共租边界的信息泄露；在IBM 156量子比特与IQM 20量子比特设备上实测表明，基准误差相同的边界泄漏差异显著，统一Pauli随机化可将泄漏降至零但有保真度代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业云量子处理器以多租户方式向独立用户出租设备的不相交分区，而云厂商仅报告平均门错误率。该指标无法反映一个租户能从另一租户处获取多少信息，因此需要一种能刻画跨共租边界信息泄露的度量，以便用户在接入设备前评估隐私与安全风险。

Method: 提出基于量子态可区分性的信息论信息泄露度量，用以量化跨共租边界的信息泄露；在商用156量子比特（IBM Kingston）与20量子比特（IQM Garnet）设备上进行测量；并采用在受害者整个寄存器上施加的统一Pauli随机化作为防御机制。

Result: 基准误差相同的边界可能产生显著不同的信息泄露量，原因是标准报告的错误指标对误差的相干性与随机性本质不敏感，而所提泄漏度量可以区分。统一Pauli随机化防御可将信息泄露降至零，理论上不带来保真度损失，但实验中因误差累积出现不可忽略的保真度退化。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁量子云计算提供商在设备数据表中除标准错误率外还应报告信息泄露指标，使用户能够在接入设备前计算隐私与安全风险。

Abstract: Multi-tenant computing enables providers of commercial cloud quantum processors to rent disjoint sectors of a device to independent users. Average gate error, which cloud quantum computing providers report, does not determine how much one tenant learns about another. We propose an information-theoretic notion of information leakage across co-tenancy boundaries stemming from quantum state distinguishability. We measure this leakage on commercially-available 156-qubit (IBM Kingston) and 20-qubit (IQM Garnet) devices. Boundaries with identical benchmarked error can offer significantly different amount of information leakage because standard reported measures of error are blind to coherent-versus-stochastic nature of the error while the proposed notion of information leakage is not. A uniform Pauli randomisation implemented over the victim's whole register is used as a defence mechanism to reduce the information leakage to zero. The defence theoretically does not incur a fidelity cost, but the experiments show a non-trivial degradation caused by accumulation of errors. We provide a specific call-for-action to the providers of quantum cloud computing to report information leakage in addition to standard error rates in their device datasheet to enable users to compute privacy and security risks prior to engagement with the device.

</details>


### [137] [Quantum Security of XOR of Permutations via Fourier Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34413)
*Wonseok Choi,Minki Hhan,Junyoung Jang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次证明了基于置换的XoP构造在量子选择明文攻击下的安全性可超越生日界，给出了完整的量子安全性上界，并探讨了其紧致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XoP（两个或多个独立随机置换的异或）是超越生日界安全性的经典伪随机函数构造，其经典安全性已确立，但其在量子叠加查询攻击下的安全性长期未解，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用Fourier分析的Polynomial Method变体：将任意q次量子算法的优势控制在次数不超过2q的Fourier分量，或构造的2q个输入输出数据上。对大多数分量给出了范数上界；对低次分量（如2次、4次项）则重新解释为其他问题的优势（如含/不含植入碰撞的随机函数问题），并用Zhandry的小范围分布进行界定，同时证明了针对大范围的小范围不可区分性新界。

Result: 证明了{0,1}^n上r≥2个随机置换的异或对任意q次量子算法与随机函数不可区分，优势为O(min{q^3/2^{rn}, q^{1.5}/2^{(r-0.5)n}, 1/2^{(r-1.5)n}})，对所有q≪2^n成立。即XoP在全部查询范围内保持安全，远超量子碰撞查找攻击的2^{n/3}界。同时给出若干启发式攻击，表明在q≤2^{n/2}和约2^n范围内界的紧致性。

Conclusion: XoP是首个达到量子版超越生日界安全性的基于置换的构造，其安全性在完整查询范围内成立；所提Fourier分析Polynomial Method及大范围小范围不可区分性界具有独立价值。

Abstract: The XOR of two or more independent random permutations (XoP) is the prototypical pseudorandom function built from permutations achieving security beyond the birthday bound. The classical security of the XoP construction is well established, but its security against quantum attacks that query XoP in superposition has remained widely open.
  We prove that the XOR of $r\ge 2$ random permutations over $\{0,1\}^n$ is indistinguishable from a random function by any $q$-query quantum algorithm with advantage \[O\left(\min\left\{\frac{q^3}{2^{rn}},\frac{q^{1.5}}{2^{(r-0.5)n}},\frac{1}{2^{(r-1.5)n}}\right\}\right)\] for all $q\ll 2^n$. In particular, XoP remains secure throughout the entire query range, far beyond the $2^{n/3}$ bound due to quantum collision finding attacks. This is the first construction from permutations that achieves the quantum version of the beyond birthday bound security. We also present several heuristic attacks suggesting the tightness of our bounds in ranges $q\le 2^{n/2}$ and $\approx 2^n$.
  We use a Fourier-analytic variant of the polynomial method: the advantage of any $q$-query quantum algorithm is controlled by the Fourier components of degree at most $2q$, or by $2q$ input-output data of the construction. The norms of most components are bounded well, proving the bound $2^{-(r-3/2)n}$.
  The norm of low-degree components turn out to be too large for the bounds $q^3/2^{rn}$ and $q^{1.5}/2^{(r-0.5)n}$. We reinterpret these low-degree components as (sums of) advantages of the other problems. For example, the degree-2 and degree-4 terms are interpreted as the advantages against random functions with and without \emph{planted collisions}, which in turn are bounded using Zhandry's small-range distributions. Along the way, we prove a new bound for the small-range indistinguishability for (ironically) large ranges, which is of independent interest.

</details>


### [138] [Excitation Gap of a Confined Chiral Mode Measures Critical Zero-Point Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34416)
*Shu He,Liwei Duan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在量子Rabi环中，手性超辐射边界处的简并手性梯子通过引入一个手性量子而展宽；四次耦合提升均匀二次项系数，产生正比于均匀零点方差与频率比η之比的能隙，该能隙按η^{-2/3}关闭而方差按η^{1/3}增长，并由四次薛定谔方程确定，经全自旋-光子对角化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究均匀与手性超辐射边界相遇时的量子Rabi环行为，探究手性梯子简并结构、径向波函数展宽及能隙标度律的来源，并考察该规律在Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya交换的自旋三角形等其他系统中的普适性。

Method: 采用二次理论描述简并手性梯子；引入单手性量子使径向波函数展宽；加入四次耦合以提升均匀二次项系数；建立四次薛定谔方程求解可观测量的解析解；通过全自旋-光子对角化进行数值验证；并推广至含Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya交换的集体自旋三角形，比较其系数与修正符号。

Result: 能隙与均匀零点方差除以原子-腔频率比η成正比，随η^{-2/3}关闭，而方差随η^{1/3}增长；四次薛定谔方程确定两个可观测量及其比值的首个修正项，与全自旋-光子对角化结果一致；自旋三角形系统遵循相同关系但系数不同且修正符号相反；该能隙与邻近的η^{-1}和η^{-1/3}规律及光子数饱和相联系。

Conclusion: 量子Rabi环中手性超辐射边界处的能隙行为由四次薛定谔方程统一描述，展示了η^{-2/3}标度律，并与相邻标度律及光子数饱和构成完整的三坐标谱图景，同时该关系可推广至自旋三角形系统。

Abstract: Where uniform and chiral superradiant boundaries meet in a quantum Rabi ring, the quadratic theory leaves a degenerate chiral ladder with finite radial width. We show that adding one chiral quantum widens the ladder's radial wave function; the quartic coupling then raises the uniform quadratic coefficient. The resulting gap is proportional to the uniform zero-point variance divided by the atomic-to-cavity frequency ratio $η$: it closes as $η^{-2/3}$ while the variance grows as $η^{1/3}$. A quartic Schrödinger equation fixes both observables and the first correction to their ratio, as confirmed by full spin-photon diagonalization. A triangle of collective spins with Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya exchange obeys the same relation, with a different coefficient and a correction of opposite sign. The ring's three-coordinate spectrum connects this gap to the neighboring $η^{-1}$ and $η^{-1/3}$ laws and to photon-number saturation.

</details>


### [139] [Near-Optimal Bounds on the Density of Low-Energy States of $k$-Local Hamiltonians and Faster Quantum Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34532)
*Sevag Gharibian,François Le Gall,Ranitha Mataraarachchi,Suguru Tamaki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 针对一般 k-local Hamiltonian 的低能估计与态制备，本文给出更快的指数级量子算法，其运行时间指数由二元熵函数刻画，在 ε/k 足够小时将指数相对 BGLGST 改进了 log(k/ε) 倍，并给出熵主导的低能子空间维数下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低能估计与态制备是量子复杂度理论的基本问题。此前工作打破了自然 Grover 界，但其加速依赖于相对精度 ε 与局域性 k，仍有进一步提升空间。本文旨在为这两类问题提供更快的指数级量子算法。

Method: 核心技术是给出 Hamiltonian 低能子空间维数的熵主导下界，通过对基态进行去极化来获得该下界；并据此构建更快的指数级量子算法；同一框架亦用于 Heisenberg、XY 与 Ising 模型的分析。

Result: 运行时间的指数由二元熵函数主导；在 ε/k 足够小时，指数较 BGLGST 改进 log(k/ε) 倍；对固定 k，所得下界在指数上最优（仅差常数因子）；并对任意相互作用图上的 Heisenberg、XY、Ising 模型给出更紧的界。

Conclusion: 本文建立了熵主导的低能子空间维数下界与相应量子算法，改进了现有最优结果，并在固定 k 下达到指数最优，同时推广至若干典型自旋模型。

Abstract: Low-energy estimation and state preparation for general $k$-local Hamiltonians are fundamental challenges in quantum complexity theory. Buhrman et al.~ [BGLGST, PRL 2025] recently broke the natural Grover bound $O^\ast(2^{n/2})$ for both problems, with the improvement depending on the relative accuracy $\varepsilon$ and the locality $k$. In this work, we present faster exponential quantum algorithms for these problems, where the binary entropy function governs the runtime exponent. For sufficiently small $\varepsilon/k$, our algorithms improve the exponent by a factor of $\log(k/\varepsilon)$ over [BGLGST, PRL 2025]. Our main technical result is an entropy-governed lower bound on the dimension of the Hamiltonian's low-energy subspace, obtained by depolarizing its ground state. For fixed $k$, this bound is optimal up to constant factors in the exponent. The same framework yields tighter bounds for Heisenberg, $XY$, and Ising models on arbitrary interaction graphs.

</details>


### [140] [Microsecond-Programmable Synthetic Quantum Matter: Circumventing the Adiabatic Bottleneck for Heisenberg-Limited Metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34559)
*Soi-Chan Lei*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出单腔BEC中非绝热Floquet协议，动态合成有效双轴扭转(TAT)相互作用，在微秒尺度快速产生海森堡极限自旋压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏观量子纠缠是量子计量的核心，穿越量子临界点可获得海森堡极限精度，但传统绝热方案受限于慢动力学与退相干，亟需快速且抗退相干的纠缠产生机制。

Method: 在单腔玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体中施加快速旋转与相位跳变，从原生纵向耦合动态合成有效TAT相互作用；发展精确有限状态自动机矩阵乘积算符(MPO)编码，将虚拟维度严格限定为O(1)，实现大系统精确DMRG与TDVP模拟；对N=10^3系综追踪非平衡Floquet TAT动力学与环境退相干。

Result: 淬火过程快速产生海森堡极限自旋压缩(ξ_R²~1/N)；协议微秒时间尺度使产生过程与典型毫秒退相干解耦；唯象模型给出最优提取时间约t≈3.26 μs。

Conclusion: 该工作为在当前腔QED平台上实现海森堡极限计量提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Generating macroscopic quantum entanglement is central to quantum metrology. While traversing a quantum critical point yields Heisenberg-limited precision, adiabatic schemes are restricted by slow dynamics and decoherence. Here, we propose a non-adiabatic Floquet protocol in a single-cavity Bose-Einstein condensate to circumvent these limitations. By applying rapid rotations and phase jumps, we dynamically synthesize effective Two-Axis Twisting (TAT) interactions from native longitudinal couplings. To evaluate this beyond mean-field limits, we develop an exact finite-state automaton Matrix Product Operator (MPO) encoding that strictly bounds the virtual dimension to $\mathcal{O}(1)$, enabling exact DMRG and TDVP simulations for large systems. Tracking non-equilibrium Floquet TAT dynamics and environmental decoherence for $N=10^3$ ensembles, we demonstrate that the quench rapidly generates Heisenberg-limited spin squeezing ($ξ_R^2 \sim 1/N$). The microsecond timescale of this protocol decouples the generation process from typical millisecond decoherence, with phenomenological models revealing an optimal extraction time near $t \approx 3.26~μs$. This work establishes a feasible route for realizing Heisenberg-limited metrology in current cavity-QED platforms.

</details>


### [141] [Quantum Entanglement in Variational Quantum Classification for Breast Cancer Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34617)
*Zineb Hazmoun,Zoubida Sakhi,Mohamed Bennai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究考察变分量子分类器（VQC）的纠缠结构与乳腺癌诊断性能之间的关系，在WDBC数据集上对比三种三量子比特纠缠拓扑（A：单次重复Ising耦合；B：两次重复线性Ising耦合；C：全连接Heisenberg耦合），发现纠缠熵与准确率、F1同步上升，但折间相关性弱且不显著，仅能证明关联而非因果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子纠缠结构是否是影响VQC分类性能的关键因素，为量子机器学习中纠缠与模型表现的关系提供实证证据。

Method: 使用WDBC数据集，固定相同的EfficientSU2拟设、COBYLA优化器与分层五折交叉验证，仅改变纠缠结构以设置三种三量子比特配置：A为单次重复Ising型耦合、B为两次重复线性Ising型耦合、C为全连接Heisenberg型耦合；以von Neumann熵与Wootters concurrence量化纠缠。

Result: 从A到C，平均纠缠熵由0.40升至0.71，准确率由92.98%升至93.68%，F1由90.32%升至91.28%，且C在各折间最稳定；但纠缠与性能的折级相关性弱且不显著。

Conclusion: 纠缠结构与VQC性能之间存在关联，但不能证明因果关系；更丰富的拓扑同时提升电路表达力，两种效应无法完全分离。

Abstract: This study looks at how the entangling structure of a variational quantum classifier (VQC) relates to its performance in breast cancer diagnosis, using the Wisconsin Diagnostic Breast Cancer (WDBC) dataset. We tested three three-qubit configurations that share the same EfficientSU2 ansatz, COBYLA optimizer, and stratified five-fold cross-validation, but differ in their entangling structure: a single- repetition Ising-type coupling (A), a two-repetition linear Ising-type coupling (B), and a fully connected Heisenberg-type coupling (C). Entanglement was measured with the von Neumann entropy and the Wootters concurrence. From A to C, the mean entropy rose from 0.40 to 0.71, accuracy rose from 92.98% to 93.68%, and F1-score rose from 90.32% to 91.28%. Configuration C was also the most stable across folds. However, the fold-level correlations between entanglement and performance were weak and not significant. Richer topologies also increase circuit expressivity, so the two effects cannot be fully separated. These results show an association, not a proven causal link, between entangling structure and VQC performance.

</details>


### [142] [Depth-Optimal Quantum Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34659)
*Francisca Vasconcelos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文实现了首个用于任意单量子比特门合成的常数深度电路，该构造完全酉且无需预置催化剂；在标准有界宽度门模型下达到Θ(log log(1/ε))深度并证明匹配下界，且证明对数宽度多量子比特门即可实现常数深度合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有单量子比特门合成方法依赖预置催化剂（catalyst）或非完全酉构造，且难以突破深度瓶颈。本文旨在探索：能否构造完全酉、无需催化剂的常数深度合成电路；并在标准有界宽度门模型下刻画最小可达深度，揭示浅层量子电路复杂度的新结构。

Method: 对任意常数δ>0，用O(log^{1+δ}(1/ε))个干净ancilla、Hadamard与T单量子比特门、O(log(1/ε))宽度的广义Toffoli门以及次对数宽度Fan-Out门，ε-近似任意单量子比特门；进一步去除Fan-Out，仅用Hadamard、T与广义Toffoli门实现常数深度合成。同时给出深度保持的实模拟（real simulation），将任意深度d的QAC电路用Hadamard、X与广义Toffoli门在深度O(d)内模拟。

Result: 有界宽度门模型下深度为O(log log(1/ε))，并证明匹配的Ω(log log(1/ε))下界；仅允许对数宽度多量子比特门即可实现常数深度合成。实模拟表明任意单量子比特旋转与复振幅不增加QAC的有界误差判定能力，将Parity∉QAC^0猜想归约为仅含Hadamard、X与广义Toffoli门电路的Parity下界，并揭示浅层量子电路层级与受限oracle族Forrelation电路层级间的对应关系。

Conclusion: Θ(log log(1/ε))深度在有界宽度门下不可回避，而允许对数宽度多量子比特门即可实现常数深度单量子比特门合成；该结果给出浅层量子电路复杂度的实正规形式，为QAC^0相关问题提供新的归约路径。

Abstract: We achieve the first constant-depth circuit for arbitrary single-qubit gate synthesis. Unlike prior approaches, the construction is fully unitary and requires no pre-supplied catalyst. For any constant $δ>0$, it $\varepsilon$-approximates an arbitrary single-qubit gate using $O(\log^{1+δ}(1/\varepsilon))$ clean ancillae, Hadamard and $T$ single-qubit gates, $O(\log(1/\varepsilon))$-width generalized Toffoli gates, and sublogarithmic-width Fan-Out gates. We further eliminate Fan-Out entirely, showing that Hadamard, $T$, and generalized Toffoli gates alone suffice for constant-depth synthesis. When restricted to the standard bounded-width gate model, our construction has depth $O(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$, and we prove a matching $Ω(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$-depth lower bound. Overall, we establish that $Θ(\log\log(1/\varepsilon))$-depth is unavoidable with only bounded-width gates, yet allowing even logarithmic-width multi-qubit gates suffices to achieve constant-depth synthesis.
  These results also reveal new structure in shallow quantum circuit complexity. We give a depth-preserving real simulation of bounded-error decision computation, showing that every depth-$d$ QAC circuit can be simulated in depth $O(d)$ using only Hadamard, $X$, and generalized Toffoli gates. Thus arbitrary single-qubit rotations and complex amplitudes do not increase the bounded-error decision power of QAC, even at constant depth. In particular, this reduces the long-standing conjecture Parity$\notin$QAC$^0$ to proving a Parity lower bound against circuits consisting only of Hadamard, $X$, and generalized Toffoli gates. More generally, this real normal form exposes a direct correspondence between the standard shallow-depth quantum circuit hierarchy and a hierarchy of Forrelation circuits with restricted oracle families.

</details>


### [143] [Log-Euclidean Rényi Conditional Mutual Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34699)
*Roberto Rubboli,Amir Arqand,Mark M. Wilde*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种满足所有理想结构性质的量子Rényi条件互信息（QCMI）推广，并给出其界、恢复性上下界及Rényi链式法则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QCMI是刻画条件独立性的基本度量，在量子信息与多体物理中应用广泛，但此前尚缺乏同时满足全部理想结构性质的完全量子Rényi推广，这一直是开放问题。

Method: 引入一种量子Rényi条件互信息，验证其在局域量子信道下单调、张量积下可加、随Rényi参数单调，并在α→1时收敛到QCMI；用相对熵对Petz恢复态建立界，并推导对Markov态与可分态集合相对熵的界及Rényi链式法则。

Result: 该量可作为近似条件独立性的独特度量；由此得到基于恢复的QCMI上下界，用KL散度的量子推广表示，在若干区间改进了已知最佳结果；并给出Rényi链式法则，其在经典情形下也似为新结果。

Conclusion: 所提量子Rényi条件互信息兼具理想结构性质，提供了近似条件独立性的新度量，并改进了QCMI的恢复性界，同时给出经典情形下亦属新颖的Rényi链式法则。

Abstract: Quantum conditional mutual information (QCMI) is a fundamental measure of conditional independence, with central applications throughout quantum information theory and many-body physics. Despite its importance, finding a fully quantum Rényi generalization that satisfies all desirable structural properties has remained an open question. In this work, we introduce a quantum Rényi conditional mutual information possessing these desirable properties: it is monotone under local quantum channels, additive under tensor products, monotone in the Rényi parameter, and converges to the QCMI in the limit $α\to 1$. We establish bounds on this quantity in terms of relative entropies to the Petz-recovered state, showing that it provides a distinct measure of approximate conditional independence. As a consequence, we derive recovery-based upper and lower bounds on the QCMI in terms of quantum generalizations of the Kullback-Leibler divergence, improving upon the best known results in certain regimes. Finally, we establish bounds in terms of relative entropies to the sets of Markov and separable states and derive Rényi chain rules, which appear to be novel even in the classical setting.

</details>


### [144] [Cubic Phase Gate with a Trapped Ion Oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34774)
*Nigel Benjamin Lee Junsheng,Eugene Koh Jun Wei,Mu Young Kim,Dzmitry Matsukevich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用数值优化状态依赖力脉冲的相位与幅度，在囚禁离子运动模式上实验实现并验证了立方相位门的近似方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 囚禁离子的运动模式是实现连续变量（CV）量子计算与量子模拟的有力候选平台，但构建CV通用门集需要非谐非线性幺正操作，而这类操作难以直接实现，因此需要可行的近似方案。

Method: 对一组状态依赖力脉冲（state-dependent force pulses）的相位角和幅度进行数值优化，以逼近立方相位门，并在真空态和低-α相干态输入下开展实验演示与性能验证。

Result: 实验上成功演示了针对真空态与低-α相干态输入的立方相位门，并对其性能进行了验证。

Conclusion: 该协议可有效近似立方相位门，并可进一步推广用于近似其他非线性幺正操作，为CV量子计算通用门集的实现提供了可行途径。

Abstract: The motional modes of trapped ions are promising candidates for continuous-variable (CV) quantum computation and simulation. However, to achieve a universal gate set for CV quantum computation, anharmonic nonlinear unitaries are required. Here, we experimentally demonstrate a protocol that allows us to approximate the cubic phase gate. By numerically optimizing the phase angles and amplitudes of a set of state-dependent force pulses, we demonstrate the cubic phase gate for both vacuum and low-$α$ coherent state inputs and verify its performance. Our protocol can also be extended to approximate other nonlinear unitaries.

</details>


### [145] [Efficient Simulation of Hybrid Continuous- and Discrete-Variable Quantum Circuits via Gaussian Decompositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34793)
*Kazufumi Tanji,Dot Belin Pio,Shohei Kiryu,Ulrik Lund Andersen,Masahiro Takeoka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于高斯线性组合(LCoG)分解的CV-DV混合量子系统仿真框架，避免Fock空间截断，实现多项式成本的动力学更新，并用于腔QED中GKP态生成的仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合离散变量(DV)与连续变量(CV)的量子系统广泛出现在多类物理平台中，可实现超越纯量子比特设计的信息处理；但其经典仿真困难，因为CV与DV系统的计算成本均随规模指数增长，且CV子系统通常需进行Fock空间截断。

Method: 将混合密度矩阵按DV基矢分为若干块，每块用高斯函数的线性组合(LCoG)展开表示；在LCoG表示下，玻色子系统上的高斯操作独立作用于每个高斯函数。作者推导了复高斯函数在受控高斯操作下变换的解析公式，并进一步将框架推广到含状态依赖高斯噪声的受控高斯信道。

Result: 该方法无需对CV子系统进行Fock空间截断，每个高斯项更新的计算代价随模式数呈多项式增长；框架可通用地用于分析混合量子算法、量子模拟协议及非高斯态生成方案，并以腔QED系统中GKP态生成为例进行了仿真验证。

Conclusion: LCoG分解为CV-DV混合系统提供了一种避免指数成本与截断误差的通用仿真工具，适用于混合量子算法、量子模拟与非高斯态生成等研究。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum systems combining discrete-variable (DV) and continuous-variable (CV) subsystems arise across a broad range of physical platforms and enable quantum information processing beyond purely qubit-based designs. Their classical simulation, however, is challenging since computational costs of both CV and DV systems exponentially increase. In this paper, we introduce a simulation framework for CV--DV hybrid systems based on linear combination of Gaussian (LCoG) decomposition. We decompose the hybrid density matrix into blocks in a DV basis and represent each block as an LCoG expansion. Within the representation, Gaussian operations on the bosonic subsystem independently act on each Gaussian function. We derive analytic formulas describing how complex Gaussian functions transform under controlled Gaussian operations. We further extend the framework to controlled Gaussian channels with state-dependent Gaussian noise. The method avoids a Fock-space truncation for the CV subsystem, and the cost of updating each Gaussian term scales polynomially with the number of modes. The framework provides a general computational tool for analyzing hybrid quantum algorithms, quantum simulation protocols, and non-Gaussian state generation schemes. As an example, we simulate Gottesman--Kitaev--Preskill (GKP) state generation based on a cavity-QED system.

</details>


### [146] [General Photon Subtraction from a Gaussian Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34803)
*Niklas Budinger,Ulrik L. Andersen,Peter van Loock*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于高斯模糊与滤波的数学形式化方法，用于统一描述光子减除高斯态，并据此优化猫态、立方相位态和GKP态的生成方案，同时给出近确定性生成目标态所需资源的普适下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续变量光学量子计算要实现通用且容错的量子计算，亟需近确定性的非高斯性来源。由于缺乏强非线性，概率性光子减除高斯态虽是最有希望的候选方案，但现有繁殖（breeding）协议缺乏实验所需的损耗容忍度，难以实际实现。

Method: 作者建立了一套数学形式化框架：对测量得到的Fock Wigner函数施加高斯模糊与滤波，以此描述一般的光子减除高斯态。在该表示下，初始压缩、高斯测量和光子损耗都被统一理解为对总体模糊的贡献，并可相互转换。针对多次光子减除情形，引入了一种介于繁殖协议与后选择协议之间的混合方案。

Result: 利用该直观方法，通过在成功率、前馈兼容性和损耗容忍度之间进行权衡，找到了对猫态、立方相位态和GKP态生成的改进方案。此外，引入期望Wigner对数负性作为非高斯性的加性单调量，从而建立了近确定性生成给定目标态所需资源的普适下界。

Conclusion: 该形式化框架能够统一解释光子减除高斯态中的压缩、测量与损耗效应，为改进多种非高斯态生成协议提供了工具，并给出了资源需求的普适性理论下界，有助于推动近确定性非高斯量子光源的实现。

Abstract: A near-deterministic source of non-Gaussianity is the missing piece to reach universal and fault-tolerant quantum computing with continuous-variable optics. Due to a lack of strong non-linearities, the probabilistic photon-subtracted Gaussian states remain the most promising contender. In combination with Gaussian measurements and single-mode feed-forward operations, they have been shown to provide sufficient non-Gaussianity at high rates. However, these existing breeding protocols lack the necessary loss tolerance to be experimentally feasible. In this work, we introduce a mathematical formalism based on the application of a Gaussian blur and filter on the measured Fock Wigner function to describe general photon-subtracted Gaussian states. Within our representation, the initial squeezing, Gaussian measurements, and photon loss can all be understood as contributions to the total blurring and interchanged accordingly. We find that this intuitive approach can be used to find improvements to cat, cubic phase, and GKP state generation by compromising between success rates, feed-forward compatibility, and loss tolerance. In the case of multiple photon subtractions, this is achieved by introducing a hybrid setup variant bridging the gap between protocols based on breeding and post-selection. Finally, we establish a general lower bound on the resources needed to generate a given target state near-deterministically by introducing the expected Wigner logarithmic negativity as an additive monotone of non-Gaussianity.

</details>


### [147] [Directional pumping of a two-level system by a fluctuation-regulated quantum source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34885)
*Sarfraj Fency,Rangeet Bhattacharyya*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 将激发源保留为量子子系统并随后迹出，可产生对目标二能级系统的定向布居泵浦；该泵浦由源的二阶矩诱导，具有洛伦兹失谐依赖和色散频移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典共振场驱动二能级系统时，仅产生饱和而非定向布居转移。作者希望探究：若将激发源本身视为量子子系统而非经典外场，是否能够出现定向的布居泵浦。

Method: 把源建模为单激发自由度（等价于二能级辅助粒子流），与目标二能级系统交换耦合。源的一阶矩给出通常的相干驱动，二阶矩给出上、下耗散通道。采用精确碰撞映射与涨落调控的二阶量子主方程两种描述，并考虑指数衰减的源关联。

Result: 对粒子数对角、完全激发的源，一阶矩消失，仅保留向上通道，实现目标单调泵浦。指数衰减源关联下，泵浦速率呈由关联时间τ_c控制的洛伦兹失谐依赖，并伴随色散频移。存在热弛豫时，稳态激发态布居可超过共振驱动光学Bloch极限1/2。

Conclusion: 定向泵浦源于源二阶矩产生的驱动诱导耗散贡献，其强度与谱响应由源关联时间τ_c调控；该机制在精确碰撞映射和涨落调控主方程中均成立。

Abstract: A resonant classical field drives a two-level system coherently and, with ordinary relaxation, produces saturation rather than directional population transfer. Here we show that directionality emerges when the excitation source is retained as a quantum subsystem and subsequently traced out. We model the source as a single-excitation degree of freedom, or equivalently a stream of two-level ancillas, exchange-coupled to a target two-level system. The source first moment generates the usual coherent drive, while its second moments produce upward and downward dissipative channels. For a number-diagonal, fully excited source, the first moment vanishes and only the upward channel remains, yielding monotonic pumping of the target. The same structure follows from both an exact collision map and a fluctuation-regulated second-order quantum master equation. For exponentially decaying source correlations, the pumping rate shows a Lorentzian detuning dependence governed by the correlation time $τ_c$, accompanied by a dispersive frequency shift. In the presence of thermal relaxation, the steady-state excited-state population can exceed the resonantly driven optical-Bloch limit of one half. Within the fluctuation-regulated master equation, this directional pumping arises as a drive-induced dissipative contribution whose strength and spectral response are controlled by $τ_c$.

</details>


### [148] [Classical simulation of amplitude-damped bucket-brigade quantum random access memories via predictable branch evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34935)
*Zhao-Yun Chen,Ming-Yang Tan,Sheng Zhang,Peng Wang,Yun-Jie Wang,Guo-Ping Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为 bucket-brigade QRAM 在振幅阻尼噪声下的经典模拟提供了完整的解析理论：证明 qutrit 编码中 K0 对角且 |W⟩ 为不动点、qubit 编码中 K0 虽非对角但可闭式求解，据此修正枝剪判据，使算法只需演化坏分支加一条参考分支，并在基准中获得最高 285 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟带噪 QRAM 的轨迹集成法可通过枝剪加速——避开所有采样故障的"好分支"终态可解析预测，无需演化。但在振幅阻尼信道下，无跳变算符 K0 在每个时间片上作用于每条分支，使好分支的终态不再自动可预测，因此需要专门刻画阻尼信道下的可预测性。

Method: 分别针对 qutrit 与 qubit 两种编码分析 bucket-brigade QRAM 中阻尼信道的可预测性：在 qutrit 编码中利用 K0 在节点基下对角、等待态 |W⟩ 为其不动点的性质，说明好分支沿无噪轨道演化仅差一个标量衰减；在 qubit 编码中处理两道 Hadamard 墙之间相位回踢式取存导致 K0 非对角的情形，给出闭式可解结构，并据此修正枝剪判据。所得算法仅演化坏分支加一条参考分支，其余开销与原全演化一致。

Result: 在无跳变条件下，好分支的支内不保真度为阻尼率 γ 的二阶量，每数据比特的相干泄漏约为 n²γ²/4，闭式解可无近似地刻画该泄漏。端到端基准显示剪枝相对全演化的加速最高达 285 倍，轨迹级测试确认所有闭式解精确到机器精度。

Conclusion: 该工作完整给出了 bucket-brigade QRAM 阻尼噪声模拟中好分支的可预测性理论，并把这一可预测性转化为高效枝剪算法，在保证与全演化同等精度（机器精度）的同时实现数量级加速，为带噪 QRAM 的经典模拟提供了可行且严格的方法。

Abstract: Classical simulation of quantum random access memory (QRAM) under noise can be accelerated dramatically by branch pruning: noise histories are sampled as trajectory ensembles, and good branches, whose routing paths avoid every sampled fault, need not be evolved because their final states are analytically predictable. Under amplitude damping this predictability is not automatic, because the no-jump operator $K_0$ acts on every branch at every time slice. Here we give a complete account of damping-channel predictability in bucket-brigade QRAM simulation, for both qutrit and qubit encodings. For the qutrit encoding, $K_0$ is diagonal in the node basis, and the wait state $|W\rangle$ is its fixed point, so a good branch follows the noiseless orbit up to a scalar attenuation. In the qubit encoding, phase-kickback memory fetching between two Hadamard walls makes $K_0$ no longer diagonal, yet the resulting structure is exactly solvable in closed form. Conditional on no jump, the in-branch infidelity of a good branch is second order in the damping rate $γ$, with coherent leakage $\sim n^2γ^2/4$ per data qubit, and the closed form captures it without approximation. We also revise the pruning criterion for the qubit encoding. The resulting algorithm evolves only the bad branches plus one reference branch and shares every other cost with the full evolution it replaces. It is validated by end-to-end benchmarks with pruned-over-full speedups up to $285\times$ and by trajectory-level tests confirming every closed form to machine precision.

</details>


### [149] [All-vs-Nothing Operational Manifestation of Preparation Contextuality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34964)
*Jayashree Karmakar,Rafiuddin Gazi,Biswadeep Chatterjee,Subhendu B Ghosh,Anandamay Das Bhowmik,Ananya Chakraborty,Manik Banik*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"奇偶 oblivious 隐藏匹配"任务，使制备上下文性呈现全有或全无的定性分离：量子协议以单条 ⌈log₂n⌉ 量子比特消息确定性成功，而任何制备非上下文模型都无法完美成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制备上下文性通常仅通过信息处理任务中的定量优势来体现，且量子理论往往无法达到完美成功。作者希望找到一个任务，使制备上下文性表现为定性的"全有或全无"分离，并使其在实验上可及。

Method: 设计奇偶 oblivious 隐藏匹配任务：Alice 编码 n 比特串，消息除两比特奇偶性外不泄露任何输入奇偶信息；Bob 给定输入位置上的完美匹配，须输出一条匹配边及其两比特的奇偶性。量子协议使用单条 ⌈log₂n⌉ 量子比特消息并满足奇偶 oblivious 约束。证明对任意偶数 n≥6，没有制备非上下文模型能完美成功，结论对任意 ontic 态空间成立，且不假设结果确定性或测量非上下文性。渐近上界由布尔超立方体上的超收缩性导出的傅里叶分析求和规则得到。

Result: 量子协议以单条 ⌈log₂n⌉ 量子比特消息确定性成功。对 n=6 与 n=8，最优非上下文成功概率分别为 4/5 与 3/4；渐近最优非上下文成功概率为 1/2+Θ(1/√n)。在 n=8 时，即使 Bob 仅被限制在 105 个可能完美匹配中的 4 个合适匹配上，非上下文界仍保持 3/4。

Conclusion: 该分离是定性的而非仅定量的：量子理论可完美成功，而制备非上下文模型不能。n=8 的受限匹配结果将该效应带入实验可及范围。

Abstract: Preparation contextuality is often manifested operationally through a quantitative advantage over preparation-noncontextual models in information-processing tasks, yet quantum theory typically falls short of perfect success. Here we introduce a parity-oblivious Hidden Matching task that instead yields an all-vs-nothing manifestation of preparation contextuality. Alice encodes an $n$-bit string so that her message reveals no information about any input parity other than the two-bit parities. Bob, given a perfect matching on the input positions, must output an edge of the matching together with the parity of its two bits. A quantum protocol using a single $\lceil\log_2 n\rceil$-qubit message satisfies the parity-obliviousness constraint and succeeds with certainty. We prove that no preparation-noncontextual model achieves perfect success for any even $n\ge6$. The result holds for arbitrary ontic state spaces and assumes neither outcome determinism nor measurement noncontextuality. For $n=6$ and $n=8$, the optimal noncontextual success probabilities are $4/5$ and $3/4$, respectively, while asymptotically the optimal noncontextual success probability is $1/2+Θ(1/\sqrt n)$. Our asymptotic upper bound follows from a Fourier-analytic sum rule derived via hypercontractivity on the Boolean hypercube. The resulting separation is qualitative rather than merely quantitative: quantum theory achieves perfect success, whereas preparation-noncontextual models cannot. We aslo show that at $n=8$ the noncontextual bound remains $3/4$ even when Bob is restricted to just suitable $4$ of the $105$ possible perfect matchings, bringing the effect within experimental reach.

</details>


### [150] [Simulation-Based Quantum System Inference with Neural Posterior Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.34995)
*Hang Zou,Anton Frisk Kockum,Martin Rahm,Simon Olsson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了基于模拟的量子系统推断框架：用低代价经典模拟器生成数据，训练归一化流等神经密度估计器，一次性学习参数后验，单次前向传播即可对新测量记录给出后验，统一支撑噪声学习、误差缓解、态层析与哈密顿量学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统模型可正向映射参数到观测，但从测量数据反推参数存在根本困难：希尔伯特空间指数级增长导致似然函数计算不可行，传统推断方法代价高且难以扩展到大系统。

Method: 提出无似然的模拟推断框架：将多项式代价的经典模拟器（如Pauli propagation、张量网络）与归一化流或其他神经密度估计器配对；训练一次即可将任意新测量记录一次前向传播映射为参数后验，把逐实验推断转为固定的一次性开销。

Result: 在Pauli噪声学习、量子误差缓解、量子态层析和哈密顿量学习上验证，涵盖81量子比特浅层电路与735参数推断；各任务均能准确估计可识别参数，后验不确定性还可诊断不可识别性并指示需进一步表征之处。

Conclusion: 该框架降低了量子实验的数据采集需求并加速参数推断，为大规模量子系统的表征与改进提供了实用路径。
