Here, we study this phenomenon for the state hidden subgroup problem (StateHSP), a quantum generalization of the hidden subgroup problem in which the goal is to identify the symmetries of an unknown quantum state. For finite abelian groups, existing Fourier-sampling algorithms use $O(\log(|G|)/ε)$ copies of the state, but whether this scaling is optimal has remained open. We settle the complexity of the abelian StateHSP in both the previously studied sample model and a new query model, which is a stronger and operationally natural generalization that provides access to the state-preparation unitary and its inverse. In the query model, we give a time-efficient quantum algorithm using $O(\log(|G/H|)/\sqrtε)$ forward and inverse queries, and prove a matching $Ω(\log(|G/H|)/\sqrtε)$ lower bound which holds even in the stronger conjugate-query and controlled-query settings. By contrast, we show that in the sample model, $Θ(\log(|G/H|)/ε)$ copies are both sufficient and information-theoretically necessary, even if one allows for arbitrary collective measurements. Thus, the quadratic improvement in $ε$ genuinely arises from coherent access to the preparation circuit. As applications, we obtain faster algorithms for learning stabilizer groups, locating unentanglement, and identifying hidden translation symmetries.

</details>


### [188] [An Optimal Quantum Linear Systems Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35660)
*Carlos Bravo-Prieto,Aram W. Harrow,Robin Kothari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了量子线性系统问题(QLSP)的复杂度为 Θ(κ√d log(1/ε))，改进了此前的最佳上界与下界，并解决了 Berry 和 Childs 提出的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子线性系统问题(QLSP)的复杂度此前上界（O(κd log(1/ε)) 和 κ√d(κd/ε)^{o(1)}）与下界（Ω(κ log(1/ε)) 和 Ω(κ√d)）之间存在显著差距，本文旨在弥合这一差距并确定其精确复杂度。

Method: 针对 d-稀疏、条件数为 κ 的 N×N 矩阵 A，在给定矩阵查询访问及制备与向量 b 成比例量子态的能力下，分析并构造量子算法并证明相应的下界，从而建立紧确的复杂度界。

Result: 证明了 QLSP 的复杂度为 Θ(κ√d log(1/ε))，改进了原有的上界与下界；同时证明任意 N×N 酉矩阵均可用 O(√N) 次矩阵元素查询以有界误差实现。

Conclusion: QLSP 的复杂度被确定为 Θ(κ√d log(1/ε))，且酉矩阵实现问题得到解决。

Abstract: In the quantum linear systems problem (QLSP), we are given query access to a $d$-sparse $N\times N$ matrix $A$ with condition number $κ$, and the ability to prepare a quantum state proportional to a vector $\vec b$. The goal is to output an $ε$-approximation to the quantum state proportional to the solution $\vec x$ of $A\vec{x}=\vec{b}$. Following a long line of work, the best previously known quantum algorithms for the QLSP had query complexities $O(κd\log(1/ε))$ and $κ\sqrt d(κd/ε)^{o(1)}$, while the best known lower bounds were $Ω(κ\log(1/ε))$ and $Ω(κ\sqrt d)$. We improve these bounds and show that the complexity of the QLSP is $Θ(κ\sqrt d\log(1/ε))$. We also resolve an open problem of Berry and Childs by showing that any $N\times N$ unitary can be implemented with bounded error using $O(\sqrt N)$ queries to its matrix entries.

</details>


### [189] [Optimal Query Complexity for Ground-State Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35668)
*Boyang Chen,Minbo Gao,Xinzhao Wang,Shuo Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了在已知能谱隙内能量阈值时制备基态的最优查询复杂度：给定期望情形 O((α/Δ)(γ⁻¹+log(1/ε)))、最坏情形 O((α/(γΔ))log(1/ε)) 两类算法，并证明期望查询数的下界与之匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基态制备是量子计算的核心任务，但其在含能量阈值（光谱隙）先验下的查询复杂度尚未被精确刻画。作者旨在确定达到迹距离误差 ε 所需的最优查询复杂度，并厘清哈密顿量块编码 U_H 与态制备 oracle U_I 两类调用的分别下界与最优性条件。

Method: 设定 U_H 为 α-block-encoding，存在唯一基态，且 U_I 与基态有至少 γ 的重叠；阈值位于基态能量上方 Δ/2、各激发态能量下方 Δ/2。算法用常数精度谱滤波器构造 purifier，在振幅放大中顺序复合以得到与基态常数重叠的态；期望查询算法反复执行"制备+一次高精度滤波"直至成功，最坏情形算法使用精度递增的滤波器并限制总查询次数。

Result: 得到两个算法：其一是期望 O((α/Δ)(γ⁻¹+log(1/ε))) 次 U_H 调用，其二是最坏情形 O((α/(γΔ))log(1/ε)) 次 U_H 调用。对 U_I 的调用分别为期望 O(1/γ)、最坏 O(γ⁻¹log(1/ε))。证明了与期望查询数匹配的下界，最坏情形下界由 Somma 与 de Wolf [SdW26] 给出。在 (N+1) 维系统上，当 U_H 的期望或最坏调用分别为 o((α/Δ)√N) 时，对应的 U_I 界也是最优的。

Conclusion: 在已知能量阈值条件下，基态制备的查询复杂度被精确确定：U_H 与 U_I 的期望/最坏情形的上下界相匹配，并在高维系统、U_H 调用量低于阈值的范围内证明了 U_I 界的最优性。

Abstract: We determine the optimal query complexity of ground-state preparation to trace-distance error $\varepsilon$ when an energy threshold in the spectral gap is known. Let $U_H$ be an $α$-block-encoding of a Hamiltonian with unique ground state $|ψ_0\rangle$, and suppose $|\langleψ_0|U_I|0\rangle|\geγ$ for a state-preparation oracle $U_I$. The threshold lies at least $Δ/2$ above the ground-state energy and at least $Δ/2$ below every excited-state energy. We give two algorithms that prepare a state within trace distance $\varepsilon$ of the ground state. One uses $O((α/Δ)(γ^{-1}+\log(1/\varepsilon)))$ calls to $U_H$ in expectation; the other uses $O((α/(γΔ))\log(1/\varepsilon))$ calls to $U_H$ in the worst case. We prove a lower bound matching the expected query count; the corresponding worst-case lower bound follows from Somma and de Wolf [SdW26]. The respective bounds on calls to $U_I$ are $O(1/γ)$ in expectation and $O(γ^{-1}\log(1/\varepsilon))$ in the worst case. On $(N+1)$-dimensional systems, these $U_I$ bounds are also optimal when the expected or worst-case count of $U_H$ calls, respectively, is $o((α/Δ)\sqrt N)$. Both algorithms use a constant-accuracy spectral filter to construct a purifier, which we then sequentially compose during amplitude amplification to prepare a state with constant overlap with the ground state. The expected-query algorithm repeats the preparation followed by one high-accuracy spectral filter until success. The worst-case algorithm uses filters of increasing accuracy and limits the total number of queries.

</details>
